((`ceyyum' eṉpataṟku ōr muṭipu vēṟṟumi [38))

avaṟṟoṭu varuvaḻic ceyyumeṉ kiḷavi

mutaṟkaṇ varinta mūvīṟṟum urittē.

itu, `ceyyum' eṉpataṟku ōr muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟoṭu varuvaḻi - annilam mutaliya poruḷkaḷōṭuvaruṅkāl, ceyyumeṉ kiḷavi - `ceyyum' eṉṉum col, mutaṟkaṇ varintamūvīṟṟum urittu - mēl vilakkappaṭṭa `pallōr paṭarkki, muṉṉili,taṉmi' eṉṉum mūvakikkum urittu (e-ṟu.)

avi `eñciya kiḷavi' eṉpataṉuḷ kāṭṭiṉām.

īṇṭuk kūṟiya viti eccattiṟkum, āṇṭu vilakkiyatu muṟṟiṟkum eṉṟuuṇarka. iṉi, avaṟṟiṟku vēṟupāṭu kūṟutum:

iru tiṇikkum potuvākiya `ceyyum' eṉṉum col, `uṇṇum' eṉaniṉṟuḻit tāṉē taṉ toṭarporuḷ uṇarttal āṟṟātu niṉṟu, taṉṉi `avaṉ,atu' eṉṉum viṉimutal vantu tiṇiyum pālum viḷakkat taṉtoṭarpporuḷ uṇartti, eṭuttal ōciyāṉ maṟṟōr col nōkkātu, ceppumūyiṉāṟpōla amintu māṟi niṟṟal muṟṟiṟku ilakkaṇam. ac`ceyyum'eṉṉum col `vāḻum il' eṉap paṭuttal ōciyāl kūṟiyavaḻi, ammuṭikkumcollāṉ amiyātu, `naṉṟu' eṉa maṟṟum ōr col nōkki niṟṟaleccattiṟku ilakkaṇam.

iṉi, ``am' īṟu mutaliya muṟṟucceṟkaḷ eṅṅaṉam muṟṟi niṟkum?'eṉiṉ,

`ettiṟat tāṉum peyar muṭipiṉavē'

eṉpataṉāṉ viṉimutalōṭu muṭintu, īṇṭuk kūṟiyavāṟē muṟṟi niṟkumeṉka. `peyar paṟṟi nillātu, tāmēyum toṭarpporuḷ uṇartti muṟṟiniṟkum,' eṉac cēṉāvariyar kūṟiṉārāl eṉiṉ, `cāttaṟkuc cōṟuiṭuka!' eṉa oruvaṉ ēviyavaḻi avvēvappaṭṭāṉ avaṟku ataṉi īttuvantu `uṇṭāṉ,' eṉṟavaḻi, `yāṉ kūṟiya cāttaṉ uṇṭāṉ,' eṉaviṉimutal toṭarpporuḷ avaṉ maṉattiṉkaṇ uṇartti niṟpatu allatu,naṉmakkaḷ kuḻīiyavaḻi oruvaṉ vantu niṉṟu `uṇṭāṉ,' eṉṟavaḻiavviṉimutal toṭarpporuḷ uṇarttātu pālmāttiram uṇartti niṟkumeṉpatu karuti,

`ettiṟat tāṉum peyarmuṭi piṉavē.'

eṉṟār ākaliṉ, atu poruttam iṉṟu eṉka.

((`ceyyum' eṉṉum peyarecca īṟu keṭumāṟu [41))

((viriviṉkaṇ kālam mayaṅkal [44))

virinta poruḷa eṉmaṉār pulavar.

itu, viriviṉkaṇ etirvum nikaḻvum iṟappoṭu mayaṅkum eṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) vārāk kālattum - nikaḻum kālattum ōrāṅku varūumviṉiccoṟkiḷavi - etir kālattum nikaḻ kālattum oru paṭiyākavarukiṉṟa viṉiccollākiya colliṉ poruṇmiyi, iṟanta kālattukkuṟippoṭu kiḷattal - toḻil iṟantiṉṟēṉum colluvāṉ karuttu vakiyāltoḻil iṟantaṉavākak karuti kūṟal, virinta poruḷa eṉmaṉār pulavar- virivu poruṇmiyi uṭiya eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) cōṟu pāṇittavaḻi uṇṇātiruntāṉip pōtal vēṇṭumkuṟiyuṭiyāṉ oruvaṉ, `iṉṉum uṇṭiliyō ?' eṉṟavaḻi, `uṇṭēṉ;pōntēṉ,' eṉṉum. uṇṇāniṉṟāṉum, `uṇṭēṉ;pōntēṉ,' eṉṉum.

TC244n

((ciṟappiṉkaṇṇum kālam mayaṅkal [45))

mikkataṉ maruṅkiṉ viṉiccoṟ cuṭṭi

appaṇpu kuṟitta viṉimutaṟ kiḷavi

ceyva tilvaḻi nikaḻum kālattu

meypeṟat tōṉṟum poruṭṭā kummē.

itu, mikkatu oṉṟaṉkaṇṇē iṟappum etirvum nikaḻvōṭu mayaṅkumeṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) mikkataṉ maruṅkiṉ viṉiccoṟ cuṭṭi - iruviṉikaḷpalavaṟṟuḷḷum ciṟanta iru viṉikkkaṇṇē nikaḻum viṉiccollinōkki, appaṇpu kuṟitta viṉi mutaṟkiḷavi - tirivu iṉṟip payakkumammikka taṉatu paṇpik kuṟittu varam viṉimutaṟcol, ceyvatuilvaḻi - cuṭṭic collappaṭuvatōr viṉimutal illāta iṭattu, nikaḻumkālattu meypeṟat tōṉṟum poruṭṭu ākummē - nikaḻ kālattāṉ yāppunattōṉṟum poruḷi uṭittām(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) tavam ceytāṉ; ceyvāṉ.

tāyik koṉṟāṉ; kolvāṉ.

eṉpaṉa mutaliyaṉa mikka viṉic col

cuvarkkam pukāniṟpaṉ.

nirayam pukāniṟpaṉ.

eṉpaṉa avvappaṇpukuṟitta viṉimutaṟkiḷavi. īvi cuṭṭi oruvarpeyar koḷḷāmal, `yāvaṉ tavañceytāṉ ? avaṉ cuvarkkam pukum; yāvaṉtāyik koṉṟāṉ ? avaṉ nirayam pukum,' eṉavum,

oruvaṉ tavam ceyyiṉ, tuṟakkam pukum.

oruvaṉ tāyik kolliṉ, nirayam pukum.

eṉavum varum.

iṅṅaṉam potu vakiyāl kūṟātu,

cāttaṉ tavam ceytu tuṟakkam pukkāṉ.

cāttaṉ tavam ceyyiṉ, tuṟakkam pukuvaṉ.

eṉac cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koṇṭavaḻi ēṉik kālaṅkaḷālkūṟappaṭutaliṉ, `ceyva tilvaḻi' eṉṟār.

`viṉiccol' eṉṟārēṉum,

tavam ceytāṉ

tāyik koṉṟāṉ

eṉa viṉippeyarāy varutalum koḷka.

TC245n

((`itu ceyal vēṇṭum' eṉpatu īriṭattup poruḷ taral [46))

ituceyal vēṇṭum eṉṉum kiḷavi

iruvayiṉ niliyum poruṭṭā kummē

taṉpā lāṉum piṟaṉpā lāṉum.

itu, muṟṟuccol poruḷ tarum vēṟupāṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) itu ceyal vēṇṭum eṉṉum kiḷavi - `ikkāriyattiṉic ceyalvēṇṭum,' eṉṟu collappaṭum muṟṟuccol, taṉ pālāṉum piṟaṉ pālāṉumiru vayiṉ niliyum poruṭṭu ākummē - akkāriyattiṉic ceyvāṉtaṉṉiṭattum avaṉ ceyali vēṇṭiyiruppāṉ piṟaṉ oruvaṉiṭattum eṉṟairaṇṭu iṭattum nilipeṟum poruṇmiyi uṭiyatām(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) cāttaṉ ōtal vēṇṭum

eṉpuḻi, `vēṇṭum' eṉṉum muṟṟuccol oruvaḻic `cāttaṉ' eṉpatueḻuvāyāy, `vēṇṭum' eṉṉum payaṉiliyōṭu muṭintuḻi avvēṇṭutalcāttaṉatāyum, `cāttaṉ' eṉṉum eḻuvāy, `ōtutal' eṉṉum viṉippeyarkoṇṭavaḻi ivvēṇṭutal tanti tāymēlum niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.

TC246n

((vaṟpuṟuttalil viṉā etirmaṟip poruḷ taral [47))

vaṉpuṟa varūum viṉāvuṭi viṉiccol

etirmaṟut tuṇarttutaṟ kurimiyum uṭittē.

ituvum viṉiccol poruḷpaṭum vēṟupāṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)vaṉpuṟa varūum viṉāvuṭi viṉiccol

etirmaṟut tuṇarttutaṟ kuṟimiyum uṭittē.

ituvum viṉiccol poruḷpaṭum vēṟupāṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)vaṉpuṟa varūum viṉāvuṭi viṉiccol - oruvaṉ iṉṉāṅkuurittāṉ eṉṟu oruvaṉ maṉaṅkoṇṭiruntavaḻit tāṉ avviṉṉāṅkuuriyāmiyi avaṉ maṉattu valiyuṟuttutaṟku varum viṉāviṉiyuṭiyaviṉiccol, etirmaṟuttu uṇarttutaṟku urimiyum uṭittu -avvuriyāmiyi uṇarttukiṉṟa nilimiyi maṟuttut tāṉ urittāṉākauṭampaṭṭamiyi avaṟku uṇarttutaṟku urimiyiṉiyum uṭittu(e-ṟu.)

ummi etirmaṟi ākalāṉ, maṟuttal perumpāṉmi; nērtalciṟupāṉmiyām.

`ā, ē, ō' eṉpaṉa viṉā.

oruvaṉ katattāṉāka kaḷiyāṉāka mayaṅki iṉṉāṅku urittup piṉteruṇṭavaḻi, avviṉṉāṅku urikkappaṭṭāṉ, `nī eṉṉi vitāy,'eṉṟakkāl, `yāṉ vitēṉō ?' eṉat tāṉ viyāmiyi valiyuṟuttutaṟkukkūṟiyatutāṉē, `appoḻutu vitēṉ; nōkātē!' eṉa nērntami paṭavumniṟkum. iṉi, tāṉ vitamiyi valiyuṟuttutaṟku varum `vitēṉē!'eṉṉum col, `vitilēṉ,' eṉṉum etirmaṟiyi uṇartti niṟkumeṉṟalumām.

((iyaṟkiyilum teḷivilum kālam mayaṅkal [48))

vārāk kālattu viṉiccoṟ kiḷavi

iṟappiṉum nikaḻviṉum ciṟappattōṉṟum

iyaṟkiyum teḷivum kiḷakkuṅ kāli.

iḵtu, etirvu iṟappōṭum nikaḻvōṭum mayaṅkum eṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) vārāk kālattu viṉiccoṟkiḷavi - etirkālattiṟku uriyaviṉiccollākiya colliṉ poruṇmi, iṟappiṉum nikaḻviṉum ciṟappattōṉṟum iyaṟkiyum teḷivum kiḷakkuṅkāli - vaḻaṅkalāṟṟāṉ `iḵtuippeṟṟittu,' eṉap peṟṟiyāṉ aṟintirunta iyalpākalum `itu nikaḻiṉ,atu nikaḻum,' eṉa nūl neṟiyāl teḷiyappaṭutalum collumiṭattuiṟanta kālac collāṉum nikaḻ kālac collāṉum viḷaṅkat tōṉṟum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `ikkāṭṭuḷ pōkiṉ, kūṟi kōṭpaṭuvaṉ,' eṉaṟpālatu, `kūṟikōṭpaṭṭāṉ, kūṟikōṭpaṭukiṉṟāṉ,' eṉak kūṟutal iyaṟki.

`eṟumpu muṭṭi koṇṭu teṟṟi ēṟiyatu kaṇṭuḻi maḻi peyvatām,'eṉṉātu, `maḻi peytatu maḻi peykiṉṟatu,' eṉak kūṟutal teḷivu.

TC248n

((ceyappaṭuporuḷ viṉimutal pōla varutal [49))

ceyappaṭu poruḷic ceytatu pōlat

toḻiṟpaṭak kiḷattalum vaḻakkiyal marapē.

itu, viṉiccoṟkaṇ marapu vaḻuvamiti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)ceyappaṭuporuḷi - oru viṉimutal oru toḻil ceyvataṉiuṟuvatākiya poruḷi, ceytatupōlat toḻiṟpaṭak kiḷattalum - tāṉattoḻiliṉic ceyta viṉimutal pōla attoḻil ataṉ mēṟpaṭakkūṟutalum, vaḻakkiyal marapē - vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇē naṭakkummuṟimi(e-ṟu.)

eṉavē, ilakkaṇam aṉṟāyiṟṟu.

(u-m) `illam meḻukappaṭṭatu,' eṉṉātu, illam meḻukiṟṟu,'eṉṟalām.

iṅṅaṉam viṉimutal vāypāṭṭāl kiḷattalē aṉṟi, eḷitiṉaṭappaṭuvataṉi nōkki, `arici tāṉē aṭṭatu,' eṉac ceyappaṭuporuḷi viṉi mutalākak kūṟalum aṭaṅkutaṟkut`toḻiṟpaṭakkiḷattalum' eṉṟār. ivi karumak karuttā.

iṉi, `oṉṟeṉa muṭitta'lāṉ, `ivvāḷ eṟiyum,' eṉak karuviyittāṉēceyvatākak kūṟum karuvik karuttāvum, `aracaṉ eṭutta ālayam,' eṉaēviṉāṉik karuttāvākak kūṟum ētuk karuttāvum koḷka.

((vēṟu iṭattum kālam mayaṅkal [50))

iṟappē etirvē āyiru kālamum

ciṟappat tōṉṟum mayaṅkumoḻik kiḷavi.

iḵtu, oru poruṇmi kuṟiyātu iṟappum etirvum mayaṅkumeṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) iṟappē etirvē āyiru kālamum - iṟappum etirvum ākiyaavviraṇṭu kālamum, mayaṅkum moḻikkiḷavi ciṟappat tōṉṟum - tammuḷmayaṅkum moḻipporuḷāy viḷaṅkat tōṉṟum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) ivar paṇṭu ippoḻiliṭattu viḷiyāṭuvar.

nāḷi avaṉ vāḷoṭu vekuṇṭu vantāṉ piṉ, nī eṉ ceykuvi ?

eṉa varum. avi viṉippeyarum viṉiyumāy mayaṅkutaliṉ,`mayaṅkum viṉiccoṟkiḷavi' eṉṉātu potuppaṭa `mayaṅkummoḻikkiḷavi' eṉṟār.

TC250n

((nikaḻvu ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭu mayaṅkal [51))

ēṉik kālamum mayaṅkutal variyār.

(u-m.) ivaḷ paṇṭu ippoḻiliṭattu viḷiyāṭum .

ivaḷ nāḷi varum.

((iṭiccoṟkup potu ilakkaṇam [1))

iṭiyeṉap paṭupa peyaroṭum viṉiyoṭum
naṭipeṟ ṟiyalum; tamakkiyal pilavē.

eṉpatu cūttiram. niṟutta muṟiyāṉē iṭicol uṇarttukiṉṟamiyiṉ,ivvōttu iṭic col ōttu eṉṉum peyarttu āyiṟṟu. iccūttiram,iṭiccoṟkellām potu ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) iṭiyeṉappaṭupa - `iṭiccol' eṉṟu collappaṭuvaṉa,peyaroṭum viṉiyoṭum naṭipeṟṟu iyalum - peyarum viṉiyumuṇarttum poruṟāc cārntu niṉṟu avaṟṟiyē veḷippaṭuttu naṭakkum;tamakku iyalpu ila - tamakkeṉa vēṟōr poruḷi uṇarttum ilakkaṇameṭiya alla(e-ṟu.)

muṉṉum piṉṉum niṟkumēṉum, perumpāṉmiyum iṭiyē niṟṟaliṉ,iṭiccol eṉṟār.

(u-m.) `a##takol tōḻi! kāma nōyē?'

`varukatil ammavem cēri cēra.'

eṉap peyarum viṉiyum cārntu niṉṟu, avaṟṟiṉ poruḷi viḷakkiṉa.

`tamakku iyalpu ila,' eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiya ataṉāl,cārappaṭum colliṉ vēṟāy niṟṟalēyaṉṟi, `uṇṭāṉ, eṉṟiciṉōrē,aruṅkurittu' eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku uṟuppāy varutalum koḷka.

TC252n

((iṭiccoṟkaḷiṉ pākupāṭu [2))

ivitām,

puṇariyal niliyiṭip poruḷ nilik kutanavum

viṉiceyal maruṅkiṟ kālamoṭu varunavum

vēṟṟumip poruḷvayiṉ urupā kunavum

acinilik kiḷavi āki varunavum

iciniṟik kiḷavi āki varunavum

tattam kuṟippiṟ poruḷcey kunavum

oppil vaḻiyāl poruḷcey kunavumeṉ

ṟappaṇ piṉavē nuvaluṅ kāli.

iḵtu, avviṭiccoṟkaḷiṉ pākupāṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) avitām - muṟkūṟiya iṭiccoṟkaḷtām, puṇariyalniliyiṭip poruḷ nilikku utanavum - iru moḻi tammil puṇartaliyaṉṟa nilimikkaṇ avaṟṟiṉ poruḷ nilikku utavi ceytuvaruvaṉavum, viṉi ceyal maruṅkiṟ kālamoṭu varunavum - mutalnili niṉṟu kāriyattiṉit tōṟṟuvikkum iṭattuk kālam kāṭṭumiṭiccoṟkaḷāy varuvaṉavum, vēṟṟumip poruḷvayiṉ urupu ākunavum -vēṟupaṭac ceyyum ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutalāyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ `urupu' eṉṉumkuṟiyavāy varuvaṉavum, aci nilik kiḷavi āki varunavum - tamakkuōr poruḷ iṉṟit tām cārnta peyar viṉikaḷi aciyappaṇṇumnilimiyavāy varuvaṉavum, ici niṟik kiḷavi āki varunavum -ceyyuṭkaṇ ici niṟittalē poruḷāka varuvaṉavum, tattam kuṟippiṟporuḷ ceykunavum - kūṟuvār tāmtām kuṟitta poruḷi viḷakkiniṟpaṉavum, oppu il vaḻiyāl poruḷ ceykunavum - nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉāṉuyttu uṇariṉ aṉṟi ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉāl kāṭṭappaṭuvatōr oppu iṉṟiniṉṟa oppumipporuṇmiyi uṇartti varum uvama urupukaḷum, eṉṟuappaṇpiṉavē nuvaluṅkāli - eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avvēḻiyalpiṉi uṭiyacollumiṭattu(e-ṟu.)

poruḷ nilikku utavuvaṉa, `ellām' eṉpataṉi vaṟṟuc cāriyiaḵṟiṇi ākkiyum, nammuc cāriyi uyartiṇi ākkiyum niṟpaṉapōlvaṉa. itaṉāṉē, `kāram, karam' mutaliya eḻuttuc cāriyiyumkoḷka.

(u-m.)`uṇṭāṉ,' eṉpuḻi, `uṇ' eṉṉum mutalnili, kālam kāṭṭukiṉṟaṭakarattiṉiyum, pālum iṭamum kāṭṭum āṉiṉiyum virittu niṉṟavāṟukāṇka.

`uṇṭu' eṉṉum eccam mutaliyaṉavum pālum iṭamum uṇarttum eṉpatu,`vantāṉ, vantatu' eṉṉum viṉikaḷāl peṟutal,

`piripuvēṟu paṭūum ceytiya āki'

eṉpataṉāl koḷka.

`taṉṉiṉam muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, `uṇṭāṉavaṉ eṉṟāṟpōlumtoḻiṟpeyarum, `nampi, naṅki' eṉṉum peyarppeyarum ivviṭiccoṟpeṟṟu varutal koḷka.

`kaṇṇakal ñālam'

eṉpuḻik `kaṇ' iṭapporuḷ uṇarttutalum, `ūrkkāl' eṉpuḻik `kāl'urupu ākalum muṟkūṟiṉām.

`aṉiyi ākaṉmāṟē.'

`ciṟantōṉ peyaraṉ piṟanta māṟē.'

eṉpuḻi `māṟu' eṉṉum iṭiccol viṉiyi aṭuttuk kāraṇapporuḷuṇartti niṟṟaliṉ, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumipporuḷ uṇartti niṉṟatallāmiuṇarka. atu mūṉṟāvataṉ poruḷ uṇarttiṟṟēl,

`kūṟāy tōḻi!yām vāḻu māṟē.'

eṉpuḻi `vāḻumāṟṟi' eṉa iraṇṭāvatu viriyātām.

`mantira vitiyiṉ marapuḷi vaḻāa'

eṉpuḻi, `muṟimiyiṉ vaḻuvāta antaṇar' eṉa aintāvatuviritalāṉum,

`iyalpuḷik kōlōccu maṉṉavaṉ'

eṉpuḻi, `muṟimiyilē ceṅkōl naṭāttum' eṉa ēḻaṉ urupuviritalāṉum, `uḷi' eṉpatu mūṉṟaṉ urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantatu aṉṟu;pakutipporuḷ vikutiyāy niṉṟu, taṉakku ēṟṟa urupi ēṟṟu niṉṟatu.immūṉṟum puṇariyaluḷḷum viṉiyiyaluḷḷum vēṟṟumi ōttiṉuḷḷum kūṟi,iṭi niṉṟa mūṉṟum īṇṭuk kūṟukiṉṟār. iṟuti niṉṟa oppil vaḻiyāṟporuḷ ceyyum uvama urupukaḷ, `aṉṉa ēyppa' eṉṉum cūttirattāṉ uvamaiyaluṭkūṟupa. avi oppiṉṟi oppu uṇarttutal,

`oppum uruvum veṟuppum eṉṟā'

eṉṉum poruḷiyaṟcūttirattāṉ uṇarka.

TC253n

((iṭiccol nilipeṟum iṭamum vēṟupāṭum [3))

avitām,

muṉṉum piṉṉum moḻiyaṭuttu varutalum

tammīṟu tiritalum piṟitavaṇ niliyalum

aṉṉavi ellām uriya eṉpa.

iḵtu, iṉṉum avaṟṟukku ōr potu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) avitām - muṟkūṟiya iṭiccoṟkaḷtām iṭi varutalē aṉṟi,moḻi muṉṉum piṉṉum aṭuttu varutalum - tammāl cārappaṭum coṟkaḷimuṉṉum piṉṉum tām aṭintu varutalum, tam īṟu tiritalum - tamīṟṟeḻuttu vēṟupaṭṭu varutalum, piritu avaṇ niliyalum - ōr iṭiccol niṟkiṉṟa iṭattē maṟṟōr iṭiccol niṟṟalumākiya, aṉṉavi ellāmuriya eṉpa - attaṉmiyi uṭiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ ellām iṭiccoṟkuuriya eṉṟucolluvar āciriyar (e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) atumaṉ, kēṇmiyā _ ivi muṉ aṭuttaṉa.

`koṉṉūr tuñciṉum' `ōō iṉitē!' ivi piṉ aṭuttaṉa.

`uṭaṉuyir pōkuka tilla' _ itu tirintatu.

`varukatil ammavem cēri cēra.'

`paṇṭaṟiyā tīrpōl paṭarkiṟpīr maṟkolō!'

_ ivi piṟitu niṉṟaṉa.

`aṉṉavi ellām' eṉṟataṉāl,

`maṉṉic collē'

`koṉṉic collē'

eṉat tammi uṇara niṉṟavaḻi īṟu tiritalum,

`ṉakāri muṉṉar'

eṉa eḻuttuccāriyi īṟu tiritalum koḷka.

TC254n

((`maṉ' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [4))

kaḻivē ākkam oḻiyicik kiḷaviyeṉ

ṟammūṉ ṟeṉpa maṉṉic collē.

itu, tattam kuṟippiṟ poruḷ ceyvaṉavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉ poruṭpākupāṭukūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) maṉṉiccol kaḻivē ākkam oḻiyicik kiḷavi eṉṟu ammūṉṟueṉpa - maṉṉic col kaḻivu poruṇmiyum ākkap poruṇmiyum eccamāyoḻintu niṉṟa coṟporuṇmiyum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa am mūṉṟu kūṟṟiuṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `ciṟiyakaṭ peṟiṉē emakkīyu maṉṉē!'

`putumalar kañala iṉṟu periyaṉ

atumaṉem paricil āviyar kōvē!'

`kūriyatōr vāḷmaṉ, tiṭpam iṉṟu.'

eṉa varum.

TC255n

((`til' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [5))

viḻivē kālam oḻiyicik kiḷaviyeṉ

ṟammū ṟeṉpa tillic collē.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.) tilliccol viḻivē kālam oḻiyicikkiḷavi eṉṟu ammūṉṟueṉpa - tilliccol viruppamum kālamum oḻintu niṉṟa poruṇmiyumeṉṟu collappaṭṭa am mūṉṟu kūṟṟi uṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟuvarāciriyar/(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `vārntilaṅku viyeyiṟṟuc ciṉmoḻi ariviyip

peṟukatil amma yāṉē.'

eṉap peṟutaṟkaṇ uḷatākiya viruppam kūṟiṟṟu.

`varukatil vallē varukatil valleṉa

vēntuviṭu viḻuttū tāṅkāṅ kicippa

nūlari māli cūṭik kāliṟ

ṟamiyaṉ vanta'

itu kālam kuṟittatu.

`varukatil ammavem cēri cēra.'

eṉa, `vantakkāl, iṉṉatu ceyval,' eṉa oḻiyici kuṟittatu.

TC256n

((`koṉ' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [6))

accam payamili kālam perumiyeṉ

ṟappāl nāṉkē koṉṉic collē.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.)koṉṉiccol accam payamili kālam perumi eṉṟu appāl nāṉkē- koṉṉiccol accapporuḷ payam iṉmipporuḷ kālapporuḷperumipporuḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa akkūṟṟu nāṉkēyām(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `koṉmuṉi iravūr pōla'

eṉa accamum,

`koṉṉē kaḻintaṉ ṟiḷamiyum'

eṉap payam iṉmiyum,

`koṉvaral vāṭi'

eṉak `kātalaṉ nīṅkiya kālam aṟintu varutaliyuṭiya vāṭi'eṉak kālamum,

`koṉṉūr tuñciṉum'

eṉap perumiyum uṇarttiṟṟu.

TC257n

((`um' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [7))

eccam ciṟappē aiyam etirmaṟi

muṟṟē eṇṇē terinili ākkameṉ

ṟappāl eṭṭē ummic collē.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.)ummiccol eccam ciṟappē aiyam etirmaṟi muṟṟē eṇṇēterinili ākkam eṉṟu appāl eṭṭē - `um' eṉṉum col eccattikkuṟippatum ciṟappik kuṟippatum aiyattik kuṟippatumetirmaṟiyik kuṟippatum muṟṟik kuṟippatum eṇṇik kuṟippatumteriniliyik kuṟippatum ākkattik kuṟippatum eṉa akkūṟṟueṭṭām(e-ṟu.)

eccam, iṟantatu taḻīiyatum eṉa iruvakittu. `cāttaṉum vantāṉ,'eṉṉum ummi, koṟṟaṉum vantāṉ,' eṉa etiratu taḻīiyiṟṟu. piṉvanta`koṟṟaṉum vantāṉ,' eṉpatūum, muṉ niṉṟa `cāttaṉum vantāṉ,'eṉpataṉit taḻuvutaliṉ, iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu. iṉi, ivviraṇṭiṉiyumetirkālam taḻīiyiṉa ākki, `iṉṟu cāttaṉum varum; nāḷik koṟṟaṉumvarum,' eṉpaṉa etiratu taḻīiyiṉa eṉṟumām.

iṉṉum ivveccantāṉ `yāṉ karuvūrkkuc celval,' eṉṟāṟku, `yāṉamavvūrkkup pōtuval,' eṉa muḻuvatūum taḻuvuvatum, avvāṟu kūṟiṉāṟku,`yāṉum uṟiyūrkkup pōtuval,' eṉa oru puṭi taḻuvuvatum eṉa iruvakittām.

ciṟappu, uyarvu ciṟappum iḻivu ciṟappum eṉa iru vakittu.

`uppoṭu neypāl tayirkāyam peytaṭiṉum

kippaṟā pēyccuriyiṉ kāy.'

`kuṟavarum maruḷum kuṉṟattup paṭiṉē'

`ūrkkum aṇittē poyki.'

eṉpaṉa uyarvu.

`avvūrp pūciyum pulāl tiṉṉātu.'

eṉpatu iḻivu.

`oṉṟu, irappāṉpōl eḷivantum collum ulakam

purappāṉ pōlvatōr matukiyu muṭiyaṉ

vallāri vaḻipaṭ ṭoṉṟaṟin tāṉpōl

nallārkaṇ tōṉṟum aṭakkamu muṭiyaṉ

illōr puṉkaṇ īkiyiṉ taṇikka

vallāṉ pōlvatōr vaṉmiyu muṭiyaṉ

aṉṉāṉ oruvaṉ'

eṉpuḻi, `iṉṉāṉ' eṉṟu tuṇiyāmikkaṇ varutaliṉ, aiyam.

`poykikkuc

cēyttum aṉṟē ciṟukāṉ yāṟē.'

eṉpatum atu.

`cāttaṉ varuvataṟkum uriyaṉ,' eṉpatu, `vārāmikkum uriyaṉ,'eṉa etirmaṟiyi oḻipāka uṭittāy niṟṟaliṉ, etirmaṟi. iḵtu,

`aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar iyalpumā ruḷavē.'

eṉap paṇpu paṟṟiyum varum. itu piṟitōr poruḷiṉit taḻuvātu oruporuḷiṉ viṉiyi maṟuttu niṟṟaliṉ, eccattiṉ vēṟu āyiṟṟu. mēlāciriyar, `etirmaṟi eccam' eṉṟamiyiṉ, iḵtu eccattiṉ kūṟām.

tamiḻ nāṭṭu mūvēntarum vantār.

`yātum ūrē'

`nāḷum aṉṉāṉ pukaḻum aṉṉi'

eṉpaṉa muṟṟummi.

nilaṉum nīrum tīyum vaḷiyum

ākā yamumeṉap pūtam aintu.'

`maṇtiṇinta nilaṉum

nilaṉ ēntiya vicumpum

vicumputivaru vaḷiyum'

eṉpatum atu.

`irunilam aṭi tōytaliṉ tirumakaḷum allaḷ;

aramakaḷum allaḷ; ivaḷ yārākum?'

eṉpatu, `tirumakaḷō ! aramakaḷō !' eṉa aiyuṟātu ārāytaṟkaṇvarutaliṉ, terinili.

`aitēyn taṉṟu, piṟiyum aṉṟu;

mitīrn taṉṟu, matiyum aṉṟu;

vēyamaṉ ṟaṉṟu, maliyum aṉṟu;

pūvamaṉ ṟaṉṟu, cuṉiyum aṉṟu.

eṉpatum atu.

`neṭiyaṉum valiyaṉum āyiṉāṉ,' eṉpatu ākkam.

`ceppē vaḻīiyiṉum varinili iṉṟē.'

eṉpatu, ilakkaṇam ākikkōṭal kuṟittamiyiṉ, ākkamum ām.

TC258n

((`ō' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [8))

pirinili viṉāvē etirmaṟi oḻiyici

terinilik kiḷavi ciṟappoṭu tokii

irumūṉ ṟeṉpa ōkā rammē.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.) pirinili viṉāvē etirmaṟi oḻiyici terinilik kiḷaviciṟappoṭu tokii iru mūṉṟu eṉpa ōkārammē - pirinilip poruṇmi,viṉāpporuṇmi, etirmaṟipporuṇmi, eñci niṉṟa coṟporuṇmi, ārāyumnilimiyi uṭiya poruṇmiyōṭē tokuttu, ōkārap poruṇmi āṟu eṉṟukūṟuvar āciriyar(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`yāṉō tēṟēṉavar poyvaḻaṅ kalarē.' - itu tēṟuvārpiṟariṟpirittaliṉ, pirinili.

cāttaṉ uṇṭāṉō? - itu viṉā.

yāṉō koḷvēṉ! - iḵtu etirmaṟi.

koḷalō koṇṭāṉ - itu, `koṇṭu uyyappōkalāyiṉāṉ,' eṉṟaliṉ,oḻiyici.

tirumakaḷō allaḷ; aramakaḷō allaḷ; ivaḷ yār? - eṉpatuterinili.

`ōo! uvamaṉ uṟaḻviṉṟi ottatē!' - itu mikuti uṇarttaliṉ,ciṟappu.

`māṟukoḷ eccamum viṉāvum aiyamum'

eṉṉum cūttirattāl aiyamum koṇṭār. atu, `pattō, patiṉoṉṟō!' eṉavarum.

TC259n

((`ē' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [9))

tēṟṟam viṉāvē pirinili eṇṇē

īṟṟaci ivvin tēkā rammē.

itu,perumpāṉmi poruḷ paṭumāṟum, ciṟupāṉmi acinili āmāṟumkūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) tēṟṟam viṉāvē pirinili eṇṇē īṟṟaci ivvintu ēkārammē- teḷivu poruṇmi, viṉāp poruṇmi, pirinilip poruṇmi,eṇṇupporuṇmi, īṟṟu aci ātal eṉa ivvintu vakippaṭumēkāram(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) uṇṭēe maṟumi!

nīyē uṇṭāy?

avaruḷ ivaṉē kaḷvaṉ.

`nilaṉē nīrē tīyē vaḷiyē'

`kaṭalpōl tōṉṟala kāṭiṟantōrē.'

eṉa varum.

`īṟṟaci' eṉṟataṉic ceyyuḷ īṟṟiṉkaṇ aṉṟi,

`vāṭā vaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭiṟantōrē.'

eṉac colliṉ īṟṟiṉum koḷka.

`yāṉē koṇṭēṉ!' eṉpuḻi, `nīyē koṇṭāy' eṉṉum etirmaṟipporuḷtarutal, `māṟukoḷ eccamum viṉāvum' eṉpataṉāl koḷka.

TC260n

((`eṉa' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [10))

viṉiyē kuṟippē iciyē paṇpē

eṇṇē peyarō ṭavvaṟu kiḷaviyum

kaṇṇiya nilittē eṉaveṉ kiḷavi.

itu poruḷpaṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) viṉiyē kuṟippē iciyē paṇpē eṇṇē peyarōṭu avvaṟukiḷaviyum kaṇṇiya nilittē eṉa eṉ kiḷavi - viṉiccolliṉporuṇmiyum, kuṟippāy varum uriccolliṉ poruṇmiyum, icikkaṇvarum uriccolliṉ poruṇmiyum, paṇpiṉkaṇ varum uriccolliṉporuṇmiyum, eṇṇup poruṇmiyum, peyarp poruṇmiyōṭē kūṭi avvāṟuporuṇmiyum tāṉ iṭi niṉṟu karutip piṉ varum collōṭu iyivikkumnilimiyi uṭittu`eṉa' eṉṉum col(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)kār varumeṉak karuti nontāḷ.

`tuṇṇeṉat tuṭittatu maṉam.'

olleṉa olittatu.

veḷḷeṉa viḷarttatu.

nilaṉeṉa nīreṉat tīyeṉa vaḷiyeṉa

`aḻukkā ṟeṉavoru pāvi'

eṉa varum.

`neṭunīrk kuṭṭattu tuṭumeṉap pāyntu'

eṉpatum ici.

TC261n

((`eṉṟu' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [11))

eṉṟeṉ kiḷaviyum ataṉō raṟṟē.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.) eṉṟu eṉ kiḷaviyum ataṉōraṟṟē - `eṉṟu' eṉṉum iṭiccollum`eṉa' eṉpatu pōla avvāṟu poruḷum kuṟittu varum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `narivarumeṉ ṟeṇṇi'

`viṇṇeṉṟu viṇittatu.'

`olleṉ ṟolikkum olipuṉal ūraṟku'

pacceṉṟu pacattatu.

nilaṉeṉṟu nīreṉṟu

`pāriyeṉ ṟoruvaṉ uḷaṉ.'

eṉa varum.

TC262n

((`til' eṉpataṟkup puṟaṉaṭi [12))

viḻiviṉ tilli taṉṉiṭat tiyalum.

itu, muṟkūṟiya `til' eṉpataṟkup puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)viḻiviṉ tilli taṉṉiṭattu iyalum - `ammūṉ ṟeṉpa tilliccollē' eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ viḻiviṉkaṇ varum tilli taṉmikkaṇ allatuvārātu(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `vārntilaṅku viyeyiṟṟuc ciṉmoḻi ariviyip

peṟukatil amma yāṉē.'

eṉavē, ēṉiya ellā iṭattiṟkum uriya āyiṉa.

TC263n

((`ē, ō' eṉpavaṟṟiṟkup puṟanaṭi [13))

aḷapiṉ eṭutta iciya eṉpa.

(i-ḷ.) teḷiviṉ ēyum ciṟappiṉ ōvum aḷapiṉ eṭutta iciya eṉpa -muṟkūṟiya teḷiviṉkaṇ varum ēkāramum ciṟappiṉkaṇ varum ōkāramumiraṇṭu māttiriyiṉ mikku mūṉṟu māttiriyiṉi uṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvarāciriyar(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)uṇṭē maṟumi.

TC264n

((`maṟṟu' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [14))

maṟṟeṉ kiḷavi viṉimāṟ ṟacinili

appāl iraṇṭeṉa moḻimaṉār pulavar.

(i-ḷ.) maṟṟu eṉ kiḷavi viṉimāṟṟu acinili appāl iraṇṭu eṉamoḻimaṉār pulavar - `maṟṟu' eṉṉum col `muṉ colkiṉṟatu oḻiya iṉivēṟu oṉṟu,' eṉṉum poruṇmiyum acinili ātalum eṉṉum akkūṟṟuiraṇṭām eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar(e-ṟu.)

(u.m.)iṉi maṟṟoṉṟu uri.

`maṟṟivām nalviṉi yāmiḷiyam.'

`maṟṟuṅ kūṭum maṉiyaṭi tuyilē.'

eṉat toḻilum kālamum iṭamum paṟṟi varum.

`atumaṟ ṟavalam koḷḷātu.'

eṉpatu acinili.

`uriyāṭāniṉṟuḻip poruḷ kuṟiyātu `maṟṟō' eṉavum acinilivarum,' eṉpārum uḷar.

TC265n

((`eṟṟu' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [15))

eṟṟeṉ kiḷavi iṟanta poruṭṭē.

itu, poruḷpaṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) eṟṟu eṉ kiḷavi iṟanta poruṭṭē - `eṟṟu' eṉṉum col`oṉṟiṉiṭattu niṉṟum oṉṟu pōyiṟṟu,' eṉṉum poruṇmi uṇarttutaluṭittu(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `eṟṟeṉ uṭampiṉ eḻilnalam.'

iḵtu, `eṉ nalam iṟantatu,' eṉa niṉṟatu.

`eṟṟēṟṟam illāruḷ yāṉēṟṟam illātēṉ.'

TC266n

((`maṟṟi' eṉṉum iṭiccelliṉ poruḷ [16))

maṟṟiya teṉṉum kiḷavitāṉē

cuṭṭunili oḻiya iṉaṅkuṟittaṉṟē.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.) maṟṟiyatu eṉṉum kiḷavitāṉē - `maṟṟiyatu' eṉappeyarkku mutal niliyāy varum `maṟṟi' eṉṉum aikāra īṟṟuiṭiccol, cuṭṭu nili oḻiya iṉam kuṟittaṉṟē - oruvaṉ muṉṉarkkarutappaṭṭa poruḷ oḻiya ataṉ iṉapporuḷik karuti niṟkum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) āṭi koṇarntavaḻi, avvāṭi vēṇṭātār, `maṟṟiyatu koṇṭuvā,' eṉṟāl, ataṟku iṉamākiya piṟa āṭi kuṟittu niṟkum. iṉicciṟupāṉmi `maṟṟi āṭi' eṉat tāṉēyum varum.

`aḵtu, avaṉ' eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉavaṟṟiṟku `maṟṟiyaḵtu,maṟṟiyavaṉ' eṉa avviṭiccol mutal niliyāy varutaliṉ, `maṟṟi'eṉa iṭiccollip pirittu ōtātu, `maṟṟiyatu' eṉa oṉṟāka ōtiṉār.

TC267n

((`maṉṟa' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [17))

maṉṟaveṉ kiḷavi tēṟṟam ceyyum.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.) maṉṟa eṉ kiḷavi tēṟṟam ceyyum - `maṉṟa' eṉṉum colteḷivupporuṇmi uṇarttum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `maṭavi maṉṟa vāḻiya murukē!'

eṉa `maṉṟa' aṟiyāmiyiyē teḷivittatu.

TC268n

((`tañcam' eṉṉum iṭiccelliṉ poruḷ [18))

tañcak kiḷavi eṇmip poruṭṭē.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.) tañcak kiḷavi eṇmip poruṭṭē - `tañcam' eṉṉum col`eḷitu' eṉṉum poruṇmiyi uṭittu(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`muracukeḻu tāyat taracō tañcam.'

eṉat `tañcam' aracu koṭuttal eḷitu eṉa niṉṟatu.

TC269n

((`antil' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [19))

antil āṅka acinilik kiḷaviyeṉ

ṟāyiraṇṭākum iyaṟkit teṉpa.

itu, poruḷpaṭumāṟum aciniliyum kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) antil āṅka acinilik kiḷavi eṉṟu āyiraṇṭu ākumiyaṟkkittu eṉpa - `antil' eṉṉum col `āṅka' eṉṉum iṭapporuḷuṇarttutalum aciniliccol ātalum eṉṉum avviraṇṭu kūṟāmiyaṟkiyi uṭittu eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `varumē cēyiḻi antil'

`antil kacciṉaṉ kaḻaliṉaṉ.'

eṉa iṭamum aciniliyumāy niṉṟatu.

TC270n

((`kol' eṉṉum iṭiccolliṉ poruḷ [20))

((pirivil acinilic coṟkaḷ [32))

`collaṟ pāṇi niṉṟaṉa ṉāka

eṉac ceyaveṉeccam muṟṟāyt tirivuḻi `āka' eṉṉum iṭiccol vantuavaṟṟiṉ poruḷē uṇarttic ceyaveṉeccamāy niṉṟatu. ivviṉiyeccamuṟṟuviṉimutalik koḷḷumvaḻi, `ākal' eṉṉum iṭiccollip peṟṟu niṟkum.

`aruḷā yākalō koṭitē'

`taṉpeya rākaliṉ nāṇi'

aṉiyi yākaṉ māṟē.'

eṉa `ākal' eṉṉum viṉikkuṟippuccol cārntu niṉṟa coṟporuḷiyēuṇartti niṉṟatu. terinilikkum kuṟippiṟkum uriya ivviraṇṭu iṭiccoṟkaḷum ciṟupāṉmiya ākaliṉ, viṉiyiyaṟkaṇ eṭuttu ōtārāyiṉār.

`naṭuṅkunōy tiraniṉ kuṟivāyttāḷ eṉpatō... tuṟantati.'

`eṟitiri imiḻkāṉal etirkoṇṭāḷ eṉpatō.... tuṟantati'

eṉpuḻi `eṉpatu' eṉṟatu, `eṉṟu collappaṭuvatu' eṉṉum poruḷtantu nillātu, `vāyttāḷ, etirkoṇṭāḷ' eṉṉum coṟkaḷ uṇarttiyaceynnaṉṟiyit tāṉum uṇartti niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.

ivi tām cārnta colli acittē niṟkum eṉṟaliṉ, `pirivilacinili' eṉṟār. iṅkaṉam nillātu, vaḻakkiṉkaṇ `āka āka' eṉaaṭukki vantu, uṭampaṭāmiyum ātaram iṉmiyumākiya poruḷtantuniṟkum eṉṟal acinilikku ākāmiyiṉ, vē/vāṟu kūṟutal poruḷ aṉmiuṇarka.

TC283n

((`au' eṉpataṉ poruḷ vēṟupāṭu [33))

īraḷa picikkum iṟutiyil uyirē

āyiyal niliyum kālat tāṉum

aḷapeṭi niliyum kālat tāṉum

aḷapeṭi iṉṟit tāṉvaru kāliyum

uḷaveṉa moḻipa poruḷvēṟu paṭutal;

kuṟippiṉ iciyāṉ neṟipaṭat tōṉṟum.

itu, poruḷ vēṟupaṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)īṟaḷapu icikkum iṟutiyil uyirē - iraṇṭu māttiriyiicikkum `uyiru eñciya iṟutiyākum,' eṉṟataṉāṉ moḻikku īṟākātueṉṟa aukāram, āyiyal niliyum kālattāṉum - `kavavō ṭiyiyiṉ auvumākum,' eṉṟa iyalpiṉkaṇṇē `ku, vu' eṉa moḻikku īṟāy niṟkumkālattukkaṇṇum (`evvāṟu niṟkum ?' eṉiṉ), aḷapeṭi niliyumkālattāṉum aḷapeṭi iṉṟit tāṉvaru kāliyum uḷa eṉa moḻipa poruḷvēṟupaṭutal - avi aḷapeṭuttu niṟkum kālattiṉum aḷapeṭātu niṟkumkālattiṉum poruḷ vēṟupaṭutal uḷatu eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar;kuṟippiṉ iciyāṉ neṟippaṭat tōṉṟum - apporuḷ vēṟupāṭutāṉ colluvāṉkuṟippiṉāṉ uḷatām ōci vēṟupāṭṭāṉ vaḻippaṭap pulappaṭum(e-ṟu.)

muṉṉar niṉṟa ummi ciṟappummi. piṉṉar niṉṟa iraṇṭum eṇṇummi.

āciriyar muṉṉark kūṟiyataṉi īṇṭum `īṟutiyil uyirē' eṉṟu`orutali moḻi' eṉṉum uttiyākak kūṟiṉamiyāṉum, uriyāciriyarum,

`neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōreḻut torumoḻi'

eṉpuḻi aukārattiṉi utāraṇam kāṭṭātu, `kavavōṭiyiyiṉ'eṉpataṉāl `ku, vu' eṉa utāraṇam kāṭṭiṉamiyālum, īṇṭu,

āyiyal niliyum kālat tāṉum

aḷapeṭi iṉṟit tāṉvaru kāliyum'

eṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum `auu, au' eṉṟu uyiriyē utāraṇamākak kāṭṭutal`māṟukoḷak kūṟal' ām eṉṟu uṇarka.

(u-m.)`kuu' eṉa aḷapeṭuttavaḻik `kikkoṇṭēviṭu' eṉat tuṇivum,`ku' eṉa aḷapeṭātavaḻi `niṉakkuk karuttāyiṉ, kikkoḷ,' eṉaaiyamum uṇarttum. itu `vu' eṉpataṟkum okkum.

iṉi, aukāram aḷapeṭuttuḻi ukara īṟāyē niṟṟaliṉ, `auu' eṉaukara īṟākiya aukāramum īṇṭukkōṭum. itu viyappu uṇarttum aḷapeṭāaukāram ākātu eṉṟu uṇarka.

TC284n

((colluvāṉ kuṟippōṭu poruḷ uṇarttuvaṉa [34))

naṉṟīṟ ṟēyum aṉṟīṟ ṟēyum

antīṟ ṟōvum aṉṉīṟ ṟōvum

`antō! enti aṭiyāp pōril'

`aṉṉā! alamarum āruyirum'

eṉa ēkārattiṉ piṉ vanta e iḻipu uṇarttutalum,

`aā iḻantāṉ!'

eṉavum,

`aiyāveṉ aiyāveṉ aiyā akaṉṟaṉiyē!'

TC285n

((ummikaḷ mayaṅkumāṟu [35))

ecca ummiyum etirmaṟi ummiyum

tattamuḷ mayaṅkum; uṭanili ilavē.

itu, muṟkūṟiya ummikaḷ mayaṅkum eṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)ecca ummiyum - eccap poruṇmiyiṉi uṭiya ummiyum,etirmaṟi ummiyum - ataṉi muṭikkuvarum etirmaṟip poruṇmiyiṉiuṭiya ummiyum, tattamuḷ mayaṅkum - toṭarāy vantu tammuḷmayaṅkum; uṭanili ilavē - aṅṅaṉam mayaṅkiṟṟēṉum taṉ viṉi oṉṟiyamuṭipu koḷḷā(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`cāttaṉum vantāṉ; iṉik koṟṟaṉum variṉum varum,' eṉpuḻic`cāttaṉum koṟṟaṉum' eṉṉum ecca ummikaḷ `variṉum' eṉṉum etirmaṟiummiyōṭu toṭarntu niṉṟu oru viṉi koḷḷātu, iṟappum etirvum paṟṟivarum vēṟuviṉi koṇṭavāṟu kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam ulakavaḻikku uṇmiyāṉum,āciriyar `mayaṅki uṭanili ila,' eṉṉātu, `mayaṅkum uṭanili ila,'eṉac cūttiram ceytamiyāṉum, ituvē poruḷ eṉṟu uṇarka. ivviraṇṭuummiyum eccam ātaliṉ, ivaṟṟiṟkē mayakkam uḷatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.

`oṉṟeṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, `vaṭukaracarum vantār; iṉit tamiḻnāṭṭu muvēntarum variṉum varuvar,' eṉa ecca ummiyōṭu muṟṟummitoṭarntu vēṟu viṉi kōṭalum koḷka.

TC286n

((ecca ummi nṟṟaṟkuṟiya iṭam [36))

eñcuporuṭ kiḷavi ceñcol āyiṉ

piṟpaṭak kiḷavār muṟpaṭak kiḷattal.

iḵtu, ecca ummikkaṇ collutal vakimi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) eñcu poruṭkiḷavi - ecca ummiyāl taḻuvappaṭumeñcuporuṭkiḷavi, ceñcol āyiṉ piṟpaṭak kiḷavār muṟpaṭak kiḷattal -ummi il collāyiṉ avvummi il colli avvummit toṭarkkup piṉcollātu muṉ colluka(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) cāttaṉ vantāṉ koṟṟaṉum vantāṉ.

`aṭakupulāl pāku pāḷitamum uṇṇāṉ

kaṭalpōlum kalvi yavaṉ.'

eṉa varum. piṟpaṭak kiḷappiṉ poruḷ poruḷ koḷḷāmi kāṇka.`ceñcol āyiṉ muṟpaṭak kiḷakka,' eṉavē, `ummiyōṭu variṉ piṟpaṭakkiḷakka,' eṉṟavāṟām.

TC287n

((muṟṟummi ecca ummiyākum vaḻuvamiti [37))

muṟṟiya ummit tokiccol maruṅkiṉ

eccak kiḷavi urittum ākum.

itu, muṟṟummi ecca ummiyām poruḷ tarum eṉa vaḻuamikkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) muṟṟiya ummit tokiccol maruṅkiṉ - muṟṟummi aṭuttuniṉṟa peyarum viṉiyum cārnta colliṭattu, eccakkiḷavi urittumākum - eccaccol urittum ākum(e-ṟu.)

eṉavē, eccapporuḷ kuṟiyātu niṟṟalē perumpāṉmi. `ēṟpuḻikkōṭa'lāl, iḵtu etirmaṟik kaṇṇatu eṉak koḷka.

(u-m.) pattum koṭāl. aṉittum koṭāl.

eṉṉum muṟṟummikaḷ, `cila kiṭakkak koṭu,' eṉa eccapporuḷuṇartti niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka. `tokiccol' eṉṟataṉāṉē, `ellārum vārār,'eṉap poruṭpeyarkkaṇṇum koḷka. `pattum koṭu; piṟavum koṭu,' eṉpatukaruttāyiṉ, aḵtu ecca ummiyām.

TC288n

((īṟṟaci ēkārattiṟku aḷapu oṉṟu [38))

īṟṟuniṉ ṟiaicakkum ēyeṉ iṟuti

kūṟṟuvayiṉ ōraḷa pākalum urittē.

iḵtu, īṟṟaci ēkārattiṟku vēṟōr ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) īṟṟu niṉṟu icikkum ē eṉ iṟuti - ceyyuḷ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟuicikkum īṟṟaci ēkāram, kūṟṟuvayiṉ - acceyyuḷiṭattup pā eṉṉumuṟuppiṉi nilipeṟak kūṟumiṭattu, ōr aḷapu ākalum urittē -taṉakku uriya iraṇṭu māttiriyē aṉṟip piṉṉarum oru māttiri uṇṭāyvarutalum urittu(e-ṟu.)

ummiyāṉ, mūṉṟu māttiri peṟātu iraṇṭu māttiri peṟṟuvarutalum urittāyiṟṟu. pā eṉṉum uṟuppiṉi nilipeṟak kūṟumiṭattumūṉṟu māttiri peṟuvatu ceyyuḷ iṟutikkaṇ niṟkum īṟṟaciyē eṉpatuaṟivittaṟku `ēyeṉ iṟuti' eṉṟu mīṭṭum kūṟiṉār.

(u-m.)`kaṭalpōl tōṉṟala kāṭiṟan tōrēe'

`akilpaṭu kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭiṟan tōrēe.'

`taṇkaṭal vēliniṉ kuṭanā ṭaṟṟēe.'

ivi pōlvaṉa piṟa cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷellām mūṉṟu māttiri peṟṟuppā eṉṉum uṟuppi viḷakki niṟkumāṟu kāṇka.

iṉi,

`avarē, kēṭil viḻupporuḷ tarumār pācili

vāṭā vaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭiṟan tōrēe

eṉac ceyyuḷ iṭikkaṇ varum īṟṟaci ēkāram iraṇṭu māttiripeṟṟup pā eṉṉum uṟuppi viḷakki niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka. iḵtuīṟṟaciyākaliṉ, ceyyuḷiyaliṟkūṟum māttiri eṉṉum uṟuppiṉkaṇaṭaṅkātu eṉṟu īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār, atu perumpāṉmi poruḷ tarum coṟkēvitiyākaliṉ.

TC289n

((eṇkaḷukku muṭipu vēṟṟumi [39))

ummi eṇṇum eṉaveṉ eṇṇum

tamvayiṉ tokuti kaṭappā ṭilavē.

/iḵtu, eṇkaḷukku muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)ummi eṇṇum eṉa eṉ eṇṇum - ummiyāṉ varum eṇṇum eṉa vāṉvarum eṇṇum, tamvayiṉ tokuti kaṭappāṭu ilavē - tattam iṟutikkaṇtokiccol perutali muṟimiyāka uṭiya alla(e-ṟu.)

eṉavē, toki peṟṟum peṟātum varum eṉpatām.

(u-m.) `uyartiṇik kurimiyum aḵṟiṇik kuṟimiyum

āyiru tiṇikkumō raṉṉa urimiyum

ammū vurupiṉa'

`iciyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum tōṉṟi'

`nilaṉeṉa nīreṉat tīyeṉa vaḷiyeṉa

nāṉkum'

`uyireṉa uṭaleṉa iṉṟi yamiyā'

eṉa varum.

`toki' eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiya ataṉāl, eṇṇuppeyarē aṉṟi,`aṉittum, ellām' eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉavum koḷka.

TC290n

((eṇṇēkāram iṭiyiṭṭum varum [40))

eṇṇē kāram iṭiyiṭṭuk koḷiṉum

eṇṇukkuṟit tiyalum eṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu, eṇṇiṭattu vaḻu amikkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) eṇ - ummiyum ēkāramum eṉavum eṉṟum eṉāvum eṉṟāvumcevveṇṇumākiya eḻuvaki eṇkaḷuḷ oṉṟu mutal niṉṟu, ēkāramīṭiyiṭṭukkoḷiṉum - ēkāra eṇṇit taṉṉiṭattē aḻittukkoṇṭuniṟpiṉum piṟa eṇkaḷit taṉṉiṭattē aḻittukkoṇṭu niṟpiṉum, eṇṇukkuṟittu iyalum eṉmaṉār pulavar - pala eṇ vantaṉaveṉṟu kuṟṟamākā,tāṉ uṇarntum eṇṇup poruḷiyē appalavum kuṟittu naṭakkum eṉṟukūṟuvar pulavar(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `malinilam pūvē tulākkōleṉ ṟiṉṉar

ulivil uṇarvuṭi yōr.'

`tōṟṟam iciyē nāṟṟam cuviyē

uṟalō ṭāṅkim pulaṉeṉa moḻipa.'

ivi cevveṇ niṉṟu ēkāra eṇṇi iṭiyiṭṭaṉa.

`māttiri eḻuttiyal acivaki eṉāa'

eṉpatu, cevveṇ niṉṟu `eṉā' eṇṇi iṭiyiṭṭatu.

`vaṇṇam vaṭivē aḷavē cuviyē'

eṉṉum cūttiram, cevveṇ niṉṟu, ēkāra eṇṇiyum `eṉṟā' eṉṉumeṇṇiyum iṭiyiṭṭatu.

`yātta cīara aṭiyāp peṉā'

eṉpatu, ēkāra eṇ cevveṇṇiyum eṉāviyum iṭiyiṭṭatu. oḻintaeṇkaḷum ivvāṟu varumāṟu cūttiraṅkaḷuḷḷum ceyyuṭkaḷuḷḷum kāṇka.

TC291n

((`eṉā, eṉṟā' iraṇṭum eṇṇil varutal [41))

ummi tokka eṉāveṉ kiḷaviyum

āvī ṟākiya eṉṟeṉ kiḷaviyum

āyiru kiḷaviyum eṇṇuvaḻip paṭṭaṉa.

iḵtu, eṇṇummi piṟa eṇṇōṭu mayaṅkuṅkāl ciṟantu varumeṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) ummi tokka eṉā eṉ kiḷaviyum - muṉṉar eṇṇummi aṭakkiniṉṟa `eṉā' eṉṉum iṭiccollum, ā īṟu ākiya eṉṟu eṉ kiḷaviyum -muṉṉar eṇṇummi aṭukki niṉṟa ākāratti īṟāka uṭiya `eṉṟu' eṉṉumiṭiccollum, āyiru kiḷaviyum eṇṇuvaḻippaṭṭaṉa - avviraṇṭu collumeṇṇumiṭattē ciṟantu varum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`oppum uruvum veṟuppum eṉṟā'

eṉṉum cūttiram, ummi tokka eṉṟāviṟku utāraṇam. ummi tokka`eṉā' vantuḻik kāṇka.

iṉi, `ummi tokka' eṉpataṟku `nilaṉ eṉāvum' eṉa īṟṟu niṉṟaummi tokku `nilaṉ eṉā' eṉṟal vaḻakkam iṉmiyāṉum, eṉāvum eṉṟāvumeṇṇiṭiccollātal mēliṟcūttirattāl peṟappaṭutalāṉum, ituvēāciriyar karuttu.

TC292n

((eṇṇiṭic coṟkaḷukku muṭipu vēṟṟumi [42))

avaṟṟiṉ varūum eṇṇiṉ iṟutiyum

peyarkkuri marapiṟ cevveṇ iṟutiyum

ēyiṉ ākiya eṇṇiṉ iṟutiyum

yāviyiṉ variṉum tokiyiṉ ṟiyalā.

iḵtu, eṇṇiṭiccoṟkaṭku muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟiṉ varūum eṇṇiṉ iṟutiyum - muṟkūṟiya `eṉā, eṉṟā'eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ varum eṇṇuc ceṟkaḷiṉ iṟutiyum,peyarkku uri marapiṟcevveṇ iṟutiyum - iṭiccollāṉ aṉṟip peyarāṉ eṇṇappaṭum cevveṇṇiṉiṟutiṇum, ēyiṉ ākiya eṇṇiṉ iṟutiyum - ēkārattāṉ varum eṇṇuccoṟkaḷiṉ iṟutiyum, yāviyiṉ variṉum toki iṉṟu iyalā - yātāṉumōriṭattu variṉum toki iṉṟi naṭavā(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)nilaṉeṉā nīreṉā iraṇṭum

nilaṉeṉṟā nīreṉṟā iraṇṭum

nilam nīreṉa iraṇṭum

nilaṉē nīrēyeṉa iraṇṭum

eṉa varum.

`iṟutiyum' eṉṟatu, `avveṇṇukkaṇ iṟuti toki peṟum,' eṉṟaṟku.`peyarkkuri marapiṉ' eṉṟatu, `maṟṟiya ellām iṭiccol;cevveṇṇuppeyar tammālē ām,' eṉṟaṟku.

TC293n

((ummi eṇṇil urupu tokuttal [43))

ummi eṇṇiṉ uruputokal variyār.

iḵtu, ummiyum urupum tokkavaḻiyum `uruputoki' eṉappaṭum eṉavaḻu amittatu.

(i-ḷ.) ummi eṇṇiṉ - ummiyāṉ varum eṇṇiṉkaṇ, urupu tokalvariyār - iraṇṭāvatum ēḻāvatum tokku niṟṟali nīkkārāciriyar(e-ṟu.)

`ummi eṇ' eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟutaliṉ, ummi tokkuḻiyumvirintuḻiyum urupu tokuttal koḷka.

(u-m.) puliviṟkoṇṭi

yāṉitērkutirikālāḷ

eṉṉum pala peyar ummit tokikaḷ orucol naṭiyavāyk`kiṭantaṉa, vantaṉa' eṉṉum tam viṉiyākiya payaṉilikaḷikkoḷḷumvaḻiyum,

puliyum villum keṇṭiyum kiṭantaṉa.

yāṉiyum tērum kutiriyum kālāḷum vantaṉa.

eṉa ummi virintu niṉṟa poruḷit tantē niṟkumāṟu pōla,ivvummit tokikaḷ,

puliviṟkeṇṭi vittāṉ.

yāṉitērkutirikālāḷ eṟintāṉ.

eṉa urupu ēṟṟu niṉṟavaḻiyum,

puliyum villum keṇṭiyum vittāṉ.

yāṉiyum tērum kutiriyum kālāḷum eṟintāṉ.

eṉa ummi virintu niṉṟa poruḷit tantē niṟṟal vēṇṭum.aṅkaṉamummi virintu niṟkumiṭattu `vittāṉ' eṉṉum viṉimutalviṉikkup puliyum villum keṇṭiyum ceyappaṭuporuḷāy iraṇṭām urupēvirittu niṟṟalum, `eṟintāṉ' eṉṉum viṉimutal viṉikku yāṉimutaliyaṉa ceyappaṭu poruḷāy iraṇṭām urupē virittu niṟṟalumuṇarka.

`kuṉṟi kōpam koṭiviṭu pavaḷam

oṇceṅ kāntaḷ okkum niṉṉiṟam.'

eṉṉum pala peyar ummittokikkum ummiyum urupum virittē poruḷurikka. ikkuṉṟi mutaliyavaṟṟic cevveṇ ākki, `ivaṟṟi' eṉattoki koṭuttu urupu virittuḻiyum accuṭṭu akkuṉṟi mutaliyavaṟṟiyēcuṭṭutaliṉ, akkuṉṟi mutaliyaṉa ceyappaṭu poruḷāyēniṟkumāṟumuṇarka. aṉṟi avi cevveṇ āyiṉ,

`nilanīr vaḷivicum peṉṟa nāṉkiṉ

aḷappariyi.'

eṉṟāṟpōla ummiyum urupum virittaṟkup poruntātu eḻuvāyāy,`nāṉku' eṉṉum tokiccoṟ payaṉili koṇṭu niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟuuṇarka. ivi ummit tokikkaṇ urupu tokkaṉa. `iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭutokkaṉavaṟṟi eṉṉa toki eṉṟu kōṭumō!' eṉiṉ, `puliyiyumvilliyum keṇṭiyiyum vittāṉ,' eṉa virittuḻi eṇṇupporuḷ tantuniṟkum ummiyiṉum coṟṟoṭarpporuḷi uṇartti niṟpatu urupu ātaliṉ,acciṟappu nōkki `urupu toki' eṉṟē kōṭum.

iṉi,

`pāṭṭum kōṭṭiyum aṟiyāp payamil

tēkku marampōl nīṭṭiya oruvaṉ'

iciyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum tōṉṟi'

eṉṉum ivi, ummi virintu niṉṟu, urupu tokkaṉa.

TC294n

((`um' eṉpatu `untu eṉat tirital [44))

ummun tākum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē.

iḵtu, `um' eṉṉum iṭiccol tiriyum eṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) um untu ākum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē - `viṉiceyal maruṅkiṟkālamoṭu varu'vaṉavaṟṟuḷ `um' īṟu untāy tiritalum uṭittu(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `nīrkkōḻi kūppeyark kuntu'

`nārari naṟavi ṉāṇmakiḻ tūṅkuntu'

eṉa varum.

`iṭaṉumār uṇṭē,' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ittiripu peyareccattiṟkē koḷka.

TC295n

((eṇṇiṭiccoṟkaḷ viṉiccoṟkaḷōṭum varutal [45))

viṉiyōṭu niliyiṉum eṇṇunili tiriyā;

niṉiyal vēṇṭum avaṟṟavaṟ ṟiyalpē.

iḵtu, eṇṇiṭiccoṟkaḷ viṉiccoṟkaṇṇum varum eṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) eṇṇu viṉiyoṭu niliyiṉum nili tiriyā - eṇṇiṭiccoṟkaḷpeyarccollōṭu aṉṟi viṉiccollōṭu niṟpiṉum tattam nilimiyiṉtiriyā; avaṟṟavaṟṟu iyalpu niṉiyal vēṇṭum - avaṟṟavaṟṟuilakkaṇaṅkaḷi ārāyntu aṟitali vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`uṇṭum tiṉṟum ūrntum āṭukam

celva latti yāṉē.'

uṇṇaveṉat tiṉṉaveṉap pāṭaveṉa vantāṉ.

eṉa varum. oḻinta eṇ viṉiyōṭu vantaṉa uḷavēl kāṇka.

uṇṭu tiṉṟu ōṭi vantāṉ.

eṉpatu toki iṉṟēṉum, cevveṇṇām, ciṟupāṉmi muṟṟum toki peṟṟuvarutaliṉ.

cāttaṉ vantāṉ koṟṟaṉ vantāṉ vēṭaṉ vantāṉ mūvarum vantamiyāṉkaliyāṇam polintatu.

eṉa muṟṟuccevveṇ toki peṟṟu varum. `ivi eḻuvāyumpayaniliyumāy niṟṟaliṉ eṇṇappaṭā,' eṉiṉ, oru poruḷ vēṟupāṭṭāṉeṇṇiṉār. itaṉi, `immūvarum variṉ kaliyāṇam poliyum,' eṉṟuirukkiṉṟāṉ kūṟṟākak koḷka. eṉṉi? `muṉṉarc cāttaṉ vantāṉ; piṉṉark koṟṟaṉ vantāṉ; ataṉ piṉṉar vēṭaṉ vantāṉ; ivarkaḷ oruṅkuvantilarēṉum, muṭivil vantu niṟṟaliṉ, itu muṭivu pōyiṟṟu,' eṉṟukūṟiṉāṉātaliṉ. `immūvar' eṉṟamiyāṉ, peyar toki peṟṟatēṉum,`vantamiyāṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉ, viṉiyum tokipeṟṟu, avviṉiyālkaliyāṇam muṭintavāṟum uṇarka. immūṉṟu aṭuttu vantaṉavumcevveṇṇām.

iṉi, `yām kaṇṭa poḻutu immāṭattumēl niṉṟāṉum iruntāṉumkiṭantāṉum ivaṉ,' eṉa muṟṟuccoṟkaṇṇum eṇṇummi varutal koḷka.

peyareccattil eṇṇiṭiccol vārā.

TC296n

((eṇṇiṭiccoṟkaḷ pirintu ceṉṟu oṉṟutal [46))

eṉṟum eṉavum oṭuvum tōṉṟi

oṉṟuvaḻi uṭiya eṇṇiṉuṭ pirintē.

iḵtu, eṇṇiṭiccoṟkaḷ pirintu ceṉṟu oṉṟum eṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)eṉṟum eṉavum oṭuvum tōṉṟi - eṉṟum eṉavum oṭuvum eṉpaṉaoruvaḻit tōṉṟi, eṇṇiṉuṭ pirintu oṉṟu vaḻi uṭiya -eṇṇiṉuṭpiṟavaḻiyum pirintu ceṉṟu oṉṟum iṭam uṭiya(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`viṉipaki eṉṟiraṇṭiṉ eccam'

`kaṇṇimi noṭiyeṉa'

`poruḷkaruvi kālam viṉiyiṭaṉo ṭintum'

eṉpuḻi,`viṉiyeṉṟu, kaṇaṇimiyeṉa, poruḷoṭu karuviyoṭukālattoṭu viṉiyoṭu' eṉṟu piṟavaḻic ceṉṟu oṉṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.

`oṉṟuvaḻi uṭiya' eṉṟataṉāl, coṟṟoṟum niṟṟal perumpāṉmi. aḵtuikkālattu aritu

TC297n

((iṭiccoṟkaḷukkup poruḷ koḷḷumāṟu [47))

avvac colliṟ kaviyavi poruḷeṉa

meypeṟak kiḷanta iṇala āyiṉum

viṉiyoṭum peyaroṭum niṉiyat tōṉṟit

tirintuvēṟu paṭiṉum terintaṉar koḷalē.

iḵtu, eḻu vaki iṭiccoṟkum poruṭpuṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) avvaccolliṟku aviyavi poruḷeṉa mey peṟak kiḷantaiyala āyiṉum - muṟkūṟiya iṭiccoṟkaḷ tām avvaccoṟkuk kūṟiyaaviyē aviyē poruḷeṉa nilipeṟac collappaṭṭa iyalpuṭiya āyiṉum,viṉiyoṭum peyaroṭum niṉiyat tōṉṟit tirintu vēṟupaṭiṉum - tāmaṭintu varum viṉiyōṭum peyarōṭum ārāyntu uṇarattōṉṟi vēṟuporuḷavāyum aciniliyāyum tirintu variṉum, terintaṉar koḷal -ārāyntu koḷka(e-ṟu.)

vēṟu poruḷa eṉṟu uṇartaṟkuc cārpu, viṉiyum peyarum.

(u-m.)ōkāram:

`ceṉṟī peruma!niṟ ṟakikkunar yārō?'

eṉa īṟṟaciyāyum,

`kalakkoṇṭaṉaṉ kaḷḷeṉkō!

kāḻkoṇṭaṉa cūṭeṉkō!'

eṉa eṇṇāyum,

`nīṅkiṉa ḷōveṉ pūṅka ṇōḷē!'

eṉa irakkak kuṟippāyum vantatu.

`eṉa' eṉpatu:

`ūreṉap paṭuva tuṟiyūr.'

eṉac ciṟappiṉkaṇ vantatu.

avar namakkut tañcam allar.

eṉat tañcakkiḷavi, `paṟṟalar' eṉkiṉṟatu.

mā: `ōrkamā tōḻi!avar tērmaṇik kuralē.'

eṉa muṉṉilikkaṇ aciccollāyum,

`okkal vāḻkki taṭkumā kālē.'

eṉa muṉṉiliyaṉṟi aciccollāyum vantatu.

`atumaṉ koṇkaṇ tērē.'

eṉa `maṉ' aciniliyāy vantatu.

cāriyi iṉ: `kāppiṉ oppiṉ' eṉa aciniliyāyum vantatu.

iṉi, urupu poruṇmi nōkkiya aikāramum,

`nēri nōkka nārari paruki'

eṉavum,

`muṉṉiyuṇṭava rurukum pacuntiṇip piṇṭiyum'

eṉavum aciniliyāy varutalum koḷka.

`taṇṇeṉ ṟiciṉē peruntuṟip puṉalē.'

`vicumpiḻi tōkic cīrpōṉ ṟiciṉē.'

eṉpaṉa, `taṇṇeṉṟatu, pōṉṟatu' eṉa muṟṟuc col `ciṉ' acikkaṇpaṭutalum koḷka. piṟavum ceyyuṭkaṇ vēṟupaṭuvaṉa ellām itaṉāṉamittukkoḷka.

TC298n

((iṭiccoṟkaḷukkup puṟaṉaṭi [48))

kiḷanta alla aṉṉa piṟavum

kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṉ uṇarntaṉar koḷalē.

iḵtu avaṟṟiṟkuc coṟpuṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)kiḷanta alla aṉṉa piṟavum - mēl cārpum iṭamum kuṟippumpaṟṟic collappaṭṭaṉa aṉṟi avipōlvaṉa piṟa variṉum, kiḷantavaṟṟuiyalāṉ uṇarntaṉar koḷal - avaṟ#aiyum accoṟkaḷiṉmūvakiyavākakkūṟiya ilakkaṇattāṉ uṇarntu koḷka(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `ciṟitu tavirntīka māḷaniṉ paricilar uymmār'

eṉa māḷavum,

`collēṉ teyya niṉṉoṭu peyarntē'

eṉat teyyavum,

`aṟivār yāraḵ tiṟuvuḻi yiṟukeṉa'

eṉa eṉavum,

`añcuva tōrum aṟaṉē'

eṉa ōrum,

`celiyar attiniṉ vekuḷi'

eṉa attiyum,

`ceḻuntēr ōṭṭiyum veṉṟī'

eṉa īkāramum,

`kāta ṉaṉmā nīmaṟ ṟiciṉē'

eṉa iciṉum,

`paṇiyumām eṉṟum perumi'

eṉa āmum,

`īṅkā yiṉavāl eṉṟiciṉ yāṉē'

eṉa īlum,

`puṉaṟ kaṉṉikoṇ ṭiḻinta teṉpavē'

eṉa eṉpavum,

`cēvaṭi cērtum aṉṟē.'

eṉa aṉṟum aciniliyāy vantaṉa.

`kuṉṟuto ṟāṭalum niṉṟataṉ paṇpē.'

eṉat toṟu, tāṉ cārnta moḻikkup paṉmiyum iṭamum uṇarttiṟṟu.itu `nāṭōṟum nāṭi' eṉa nīṇṭum niṟkum.

`ā' eṉpatu, viyappu uḷvaḻiyum maṟuttal uḷvaḻiyum poruḷuṇarttutalum; `ai' eṉṟatu, ici uḷvaḻiyum varuttam uḷvaḻiyumporuḷ uṇarttutalum koḷka.

poḷḷeṉa, pommeṉa, katumeṉa _ ivi virivu uṇarttiṉa.

`kommeṉa' eṉpatu, perukkam eṉṉum kuṟippu uṇarttiṟṟu.

`āṉam, ēṉam, ōṉam' eṉpaṉa eḻuttuc cāriyi.

`epporuḷ ettaṉmit tāyiṉum' eṉa varum ekara viṉāvum koḷka.

`aṅku, iṅku, uṅku, eṅku' eṉa eḻuttiṉuḷ `iṭapporuḷ uṇarttum'eṉṟaṉavum, ivi nīṇṭu varuvaṉavum, piṟavāṟṟāṉ varuvaṉavumitaṉāṟkoḷka.

((iṭi iyal muṟṟum.))

((8. uri iyal))

TC299n

((uriccoṟkup potu ilakkaṇam [1))

uriccoṟ kiḷavi virikkuṅ kāli
iciyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum tōṉṟip
peyariṉum viṉiyiṉum meytaṭu māṟi
orucoṟ palaporuṭ kurimi tōṉṟiṉum
palacol oruporuṭ kurimi tōṉṟiṉum
payilā tavaṟṟip payiṉṟavi cārttit
tattam marapiṟ ceṉṟunili maruṅkiṉ
eccol lāyiṉum poruḷvēṟu kiḷattal.

eṉpatu cūttiram. itu, tamakku iyalpu illā iṭiccol pōlātu,ici kuṟippup paṇpu eṉṉum poruṭku uriyavāy varutaliṉ, `uriccolōttu' eṉṉum peyarttu āyiṟṟu. īṟu paṟṟip pala poruḷ viḷakkalumurupu ēṟṟalum iṉṟip peyariyum viṉiyiyum cārntu poruṭkuṇattiviḷakkaliṉ, uriccol, peyariṉ vēṟu eṉṟu uṇarka. ic cūttiram,uriccoṟku ellām potu ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) uriccoṟkiḷavi virikkuṅkāli - uriccollākiya collivirittu uṇarttumiṭattu, oru coṟ pala poruṭku urimi tōṉṟiṉum palacol oru poruṭku urimi tōṉṟiṉum - aḵtu oru col oru poruṭkuurittāy varātu pala poruṭku urittāy mayaṅki variṉum pala col oruporuṭku uriyavāy mayaṅki variṉum, iciyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉumtōṉṟip peyariṉum viṉiyiṉum mey taṭumāṟit tat tam marapiṟ ceṉṟunili maruṅkiṉ - icip poruṇmikkaṇṇum maṉattāṟkuṟittu uṇarumporuṇmikkaṇṇum paṇpup poruṇmikkaṇṇum veḷippaṭṭup peyar viṉikaḷpōṉṟum avaṟṟiṟku mutalniliyāyum tam urupu taṭumāṟit tattamakkuuriya muṟimiyāl ceṉṟu niṟkum nilikkaḷaṅkalālē, eccollāyiṉumpayilātavaṟṟip payiṉṟavi cārttip poruḷ vēṟu kiḷattal - kēṭpōṉālporuḷ uṇarappaṭāta evvakippaṭṭa collāyiṉum payilappaṭāta collippayiṉṟavaṟṟōṭu cērttip poruḷi vēṟu vēṟu colluka(e-ṟu.)

`atu mayaṅki variṉum, nilikkaḷaṅkaḷālē cērtti vēṟu vēṟukiḷattal,' eṉa viṉi muṭipu ceyka.

paṇpu, poṟiyāṉ aṟiyappaṭum kuṇam.

kaṟuppu, peyarppōli.

tava, viṉippōli.

tuvittal, peyarkku mutal nili.

tāvāta, viṉikku mutal nili.

palavum ōtiṉārēṉum, uriccoṟku ilakkaṇam muvakip poruḷum paṟṟivarutalēyām.

TC300n

((veḷippaṭi allā uriccoṟkē poruḷ kūṟutal [2))

veḷippaṭu collē kiḷattal vēṇṭā;

veḷippaṭa vārā uriccol mēṉa.

iḵtu, uriccoṟkaḷuḷ yāṉ kūṟappaṭuvaṉa ivi eṉkṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) veḷippaṭu collē kiḷattal vēṇṭā - poruḷ pulappaṭṭauriccoṟkaḷ ellārum aṟitaliṉ eṭuttu ōtip poruḷ uṇarttutal vēṇṭā;veḷippaṭa vārā uriccol mēṉa - poruḷ pulappaṭa vārāta uriccolmēlaṉa yāṉ poruḷ pulappaṭa ōtutal(e-ṟu.)

TC301n

((`uṟu, tava, naṉi' eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

avitām,

uṟutava naṉiyeṉa varūum mūṉṟum

mikuti ceyyum poruḷa eṉpa.

itu, kuṟippup paṟṟi varum uriccol poruḷ paṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) avitām - `veḷippaṭa vārāta uriccoṟkaḷtām yāvi?' eṉiṉ,uṟu tava naṉi eṉa varūum mūṉṟum - uṟuveṉat tavaveṉa naṉiyeṉavarukiṉṟa mūṉṟu uriccoṟkaḷum, `mikuti' eṉṉum uriccol kāṭṭumkuṟippup poruḷi uṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar(e-ṟu.)

kuṟippuccol parappu uṭimiyiṉ, muṟkūṟiṉār.

(u-m.) `uṟukāl oṟṟa olki āmpal'

`īyātu vīyum uyirtavap palavē.'

`vantunaṉi varuntiṉi vāḻiya neñcē!'

eṉa varum.

TC302n

((`uru, puri' eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ [4))

uruvuṭ kākum; puriuyar vākum.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.)uru uṭku ākum - `uru' eṉṉum uriccol `uṭkutal' eṉṉumuriccollatu kuṟippupporuṭṭām; puri uyarvu ākum - `puri' eṉṉumuriccol `uyarvu' eṉṉum uriccollatu kuṟippup poruṭṭām(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `urukeḻu ñāyiṟ ṟoṇkatir micinta'

`puriya maṉṟa puriyōr kēṇmi.'

eṉa varum.

`uruvak kutiri maḻavar ōṭṭiya'

`uruvameṉ ṟuritti yāyiṉ'

eṉa `uru' vaṭivaḻakiyum,

`puritīr kēḷvip pulava rāṉa'

eṉap `puri' kuṟṟattiyum uṇarttutal,

`kūṟiya kiḷavip poruḷnili alla.'

eṉṉum cūttirattāṟkoḷka.

TC303n

((`kuru, keḻu' eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ [5))

kuruvum keḻuvum niṟaṉā kummē.

itu, paṇpu.

(i-ḷ.)kuruvum keḻuvum niṟaṉ ākummē - `kuru' eṉṉum uriccollum`keḻu' eṉṉum uriccollum `niṟaṉ' eṉṉum uriccollatu paṇpupporuḷām(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `kurumaṇit tāli'

`naṟuñcāntu pularnta kēḻkiḷar akalam'

eṉa varum.

`kurūuttuḷi poḻinta'

`kurūukkaṇ iṟaṭip pommal'

eṉak `kuru' nīṭalum, `keḻu' kēḻ eṉa niṭalum īṟu keṭutalum,

`eḻuttuppirin ticittal ivaṇiyal piṉṟē.'

eṉpataṉāl koḷka. `keḻu' eṉṟu varum iṭam uḷatāyiṉ koḷka. ikkeḻuporuttatti uṇarttutalum, `keḻumutal' eṉṉum vaḻakkiṟkumutalniliyāy kiṟṟalum `kūṟiya kiḷavi' eṉpataṉāl koḷka. mēlvarumcūttiraṅkaṭkum ivvāṟē poruḷ kūṟuka.

((`atirvu, vitirppu' eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ [18))

(i-ḷ.)atirvum vitirppum naṭukkam ceyyum - atirvum vitirppumnaṭukkamākiya kuṟippu uṇarttum (e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `atira varuvatōr nōy'

`vitiṟppuṟa vaṟiyā vēmak kāppiṉi'

eṉa varum.

TC317n

((`vārtal, pōkal, oḻukal' eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ [19))

vārtal pōkal oḻukal muṉṟum

nērpum neṭumiyum ceyyum poruḷa.

itu paṇpu.

(i-ḷ.) vārtal pēkal oḻukal mūṉṟum nērpum neṭumiyum ceyyumporuḷa - `vārtal, pōkal, oḻukal' eṉṉum mūṉṟu collum nērmiyumneṭumiyum uṇarttum paṇpiṉit tamakkup poruḷāka uṭiya(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `vārntilaṅku viyeyiṟṟuc ciṉmoḻi

ariviyi'

`teḷḷaṟal taḻīiya vārmaṇal aṭikari'

eṉavum,

`pōkukoṭi maruṅkul'

`tirikāy viṭattaroṭu kāruṭi pōki'

eṉavum,

`oḻukukoṭi maruṅkul'

`mālvari oḻukiya vāḻi'

eṉavum varum.

TC318n

((`tīrtal, tīrtal' eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ [20))

tīrtalum tīrtalum viṭaṟporuṭ ṭākum.

itu, kuṟippu.

(i-ḷ.) tīrtalum tīrtalum viṭaṟporuṭṭu ākum - tīrtalum tīrtalum`viṭal' eṉṉum uriccollatu kuṟippup poruṇmi uṭittām(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`tuṇiyiṟ ṟīnta kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉi'

`naṅkiyic ceṟṟa tīṅkut

tīrttunīr koṇmiṉ'

eṉa varum.

itaṉāṉē, `viṭal' eṉṉum uriccol aṭiyākap piṟakkum peyarumviṉiyum taṉ viṉiyum piṟa viṉiyum paṟṟip piṟakkum eṉpatuuṇarttaṟku `viṭaṟporuṭṭu' eṉṟār. `iyipu' eṉpataṟku `iyippu' eṉavaḻaṅkātu, `iyikka, iyivikka' eṉa vēṟupaṭṭu vaḻaṅkutaliṉ, taṉviṉi piṟa viṉiyuṭaṉ ōtārāyiṉār.

`oṉṟeṉa muṭitta'lāl, `piḷattal, aṇaṅkal' eṉṟāṟpōlat taṉ viṉipiṟa viṉikkup potuvāy varuvaṉavum koḷka. ippiṟaviṉi,`tīrvittal, tīrppittal' eṉa vāypāṭu vēṟupaṭṭu ikkālattuvaḻaṅkumāṟum uṇarka.

((`piṇi, pēṇ' eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ [40))

(u-m.) `arumpiṉi akaṟṟi vēṭṭa ñāṭpiṉum'

`yāṉum pēṇiṉa ṉallaṉō makiḻna!'

`peṟṟōṟ peṭkum piṇiyi ākeṉa'

eṉpuḻik `kaṇavaṉippuṟantarum viruppatti uṭiya āvāy,' eṉap`piṇi' virappattiṟku vantatu.

`amarp pēṇiyum āvuti aruttiyum'

itu viruppam uṇarttiṟṟu.

`kāytalum uvattalum piritalum peṭṭalum'

TC349n

((`kampali, cummi, kali, aḻuṅkal'_ poruḷ [51))

kampali cummi kaliyē aḻuṅkal

eṉṟivi nāṉkum aravap poruḷa.

iḵtu, ici.

(i-ḷ.)kampali cummi kaliyē aḻuṅkal eṉṟu ivi nāṉkum aravapporuḷa - `kampali , cummi, kali, aḻuṅkal' eṉṟu collappaṭṭaiṉnāṉkum aravamākiya icip poruṇmiyi uṇarttum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`kaḷiṟukavar kampali pōla'

`taḷimaḻi poḻiyum taṇparaṅ kuṉiṟil

kalikoḷ cummi olikoḷ āyam'

`kalikoḷ āyam malitoku peṭutta'

`uyavuppuṇarn taṉṟiv vaḻuṅkal ūrē.'

eṉa varum.

`kalikoḷ cummi' eṉpataṉuḷ `kali' ceṟukkiṉi uṇarttutal`kūṟiya kiḷavi' eṉpataṉāl koḷka.

TC350n

((`aḻuṅkal' irakkamum kēṭum uṇarttal [52))

avaṟṟuḷ,

aḻuṅkal irakkamum kēṭum ākum.

iḵtu, eytiyataṉ mēl ciṟappu viti.

(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ aḻuṅkal irakkamum kēṭum ākum - annāṉkaṉuḷ`aḻuṅkal' aravamē ṉē/ṟi irakkamum kēṭumākiya kuṟippumuṇarttum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`paḻaṅka ṇōṭṭamum naliya

aḻuṅkiṉe ṉalvaṉō ayarntaṉaṉ maṉaṉē.'

`kuṇaṉaḻuṅkak

kuṟṟam uḻiniṉṟu kūṟum ciṟiyavarkaṭku'

eṉa varum.

TC351n

((`kaḻumu' eṉpataṉ poruḷ [53))

((`tuvittal, cilittal, iyampal, iraṅkal' _ poruḷ [60))

(u-m.) `muracukaṭip pikuppavum vālvaḷi tuvippavum'

TC359n

((`iraṅkal' kaḻivākiya kuṟippum uṇarttal [61))

avaṟṟuḷ,

iḵtu, eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappu viti.

(i-ḷ.)avaṟṟuḷ iraṅkal kaḻinta poruṭṭum ākum - annāṉkaṉuḷ`iraṅkal' iciyē aṉṟi oru poruḷatu kaḻivāl piṟanta varuttamākiyakuṟippum uṇarttum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`ceytiraṅ kāviṉic cēṇviḷaṅ kumpukaḻ'

eṉa varum. iḵtu,

`iṉiniṉin tirakka mākiṉṟu'

eṉa `irakkam' eṉavum niṟkum.

ummi, iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu.

TC360n

((`ilampāṭu, oṟkam' eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ [62))

ilampā ṭoṟkam āyiraṇṭum varumi.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.) ilampāṭu - `ilam' eṉṉum col kuṟippuccol taṉmippaṭṭu`iṉmi' eṉṉum uriccollāy niṉṟatuvum, oṟkam - `oṟkam' eṉṉumuriccollum, āyiraṇṭum vaṟumi ākiya avviraṇṭum `vaṟumi' eṉṉumuriccoṟpeyaratu kuṟippu uṇarttum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `ilampaṭu pulavar ēṟṟakin niṟiya'

`okkal oṟkam coliyat taṉṉūr'

eṉa varum.

`ilampaṭu pulavar' eṉpataṟku `illāmi uṇṭākiṉṟa pulavar' eṉaalvaḻiyākap poruḷ urikka. itaṟku uriyāciriyar, `ilattālpaṟṟappaṭum pulavar' eṉa vēṟṟumiyākap poruḷ kūṟiṉārāl eṉiṉ,āciriyar,

`alvaḻi ellām melleḻut tākum.'

eṉa alvaḻiyē kūṟat toṭaṅki,

`akameṉ kiḷavikkuk kimuṉ variṉ'

eṉap paṇputtokiyum,

`ilameṉ kiḷavikku'

eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ varum uriccollum kūṟi, piṉṉar eṇṇuppeyarumaḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum kūṟi, `paṭarkkip peyarum' eṉṉumcūttirattiṉ `vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ' eṉa mīṭṭum vēṟṟumiyi eṭuttuōtiṉamiyiṉ, `ilam' eṉpataṉi vēṟṟumi eṉṟal poruntāmi uṇarka.

`ilam' eṉṉum col, `yām poruḷ ilam,' eṉa muṟṟuc collāyum,orukāl uriccollāyum niṟkum eṉpatu uṇarttutaṟku `ilattiṟku uriccoltaṉmippaṭṭu niṟkumiṭattu' eṉṟār.

`ilameṉ kiḷavikkup paṭuvaru kāli'

eṉa nilimoḻi varumoḻi ceytu muṉṉar āciriyar puṇarttamiyiṉ,`ilampāṭu' eṉa oru collāka ōtāmi uṇarka.

``ilampāṭu nāṇut tarum'

eṉṟatō?' eṉiṉ, `illāmi uṇṭātal nāṇuttarum,' eṉap poruḷkūṟikkoḷka.

((`paravu, paḻiccu' eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ [84))

paravum paḻiccum vaḻuttiṉ poruḷa.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.) paravum paḻiccum vaḻuttiṉ poruḷa - paravum paḻiccum`vaḻuttutal' eṉṉum colliṉatu kuṟippup poruḷi uṭiya(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `kallē paraviṉ allatu

nellukuttup paravum kaṭavuḷum ilavē.'

`ceṟivaḷi viṟaliyar kitoḻūup paḻicci

vaṟituneṟi orīi'

eṉa varum.

TC383n

((`kaṭi' eṉpataṉ poruḷ [85))

kaṭiyeṉ kiḷavi

varivē kūrmi kāppē putumi

virivē viḷakkam mikuti ciṟappē

accam muṉtēṟ ṟāyī rintum

meyppaṭat tōṉṟum poruṭṭā kummē.

itu, perumpāṉmi kuṟippum ciṟupāṉmi paṇpum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)kaṭi eṉ kiḷavi varivē kūrmi kāppē putumi virivēviḷakkam mikuti ciṟappē accam muṉ tēṟṟu āyīrintum meyppaṭattōṉṟum poruṭṭu ākummē - `kaṭi' eṉṉum col `varivu, kūrmi, kāppu,putumi, virivu, viḷakkam, mikuti, ciṟappu, accam, teyvammutaliyavaṟṟi muṉ niṉṟu teḷivittal' eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟa appattuccollum meymippaṭat tōṉṟum poruṇmiyiṉi uṭittu(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`kaṭinta kaṭintorār ceytārkku'

`kaṭinuṉip pakaḻi'

`kaṭimaram taṭiyum ōci'

`kaṭiyuṇ kaṭavuṭ kiṭṭa ceḻuṅkural'

`kaṭuntēr kuḻitta ñeḷḷa lāṅkaṇ'

`kaṭumpakal ñāyiṟu'

`kaṭuṅkāl oṟṟaliṉ cuṭarciṟan turuttu'

`ampu tuñcum kaṭiyaraṇāl'

`kaṭiyurumiṉ uraṟik kaṭippuc cērpu

kaṭiya maṉṟaniṉ taḻaṅkukural muracam'

`koṭuñcuḻip pukāart teyvam nōkkik

kaṭuñcūḷ tarukuvaṉ niṉakkē kāṉal'

eṉa varum. ivvuriccol, perumpāṉmi tirintu niṟkum.

TC384n

((`kaṭi' eṉpatu vēṟu poruḷum uṇarttutal [86))

aiyamum karippum ākalum urittē.

iḵtu, eytiyataṉ mēl ciṟappu viti.

(i-ḷ.) aiyamum karippum ākalum urittē - `kaṭi' eṉṉum colmuṟkūṟiya pattumē aṉṟic ciṟupāṉmi aiyamākiya kuṟippumkarippākiya paṇpum uṇarttutaṟkum urittu(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`kaṭuttaṉaḷ allaḷō aṉṉi?'

`kaṭimiḷaku tiṉṟa kallā manti'

eṉa varum.

((`ai' eṉpataṉ poruḷ [87))

aiviyap pākum.

(u-m.)`aitē kāmam yāṉē'

eṉa varum.

muṉivumuṉi vākum.

TC389n

((uriccoṟkaḷukkup puṟaṉaṭi [91))

meypeṟak kiḷanta uriccol ellām

muṉṉum piṉṉum varupavi nāṭi

otta moḻiyāl puṇarttaṉar uṇarttal;

tattam marapiṉ tōṉṟumaṉ poruḷē.

iḵtu uriccoṟkellām puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) mey peṟak kiḷanta uriccol ellām - `orucoṟ palaporuṭkurimi tōṉṟiyum, palacol oru poruṭ kurimi tōṉṟiyum varum,' eṉairu kūṟu ceytu poruḷ peṟak kūṟappaṭṭa uriccol ellāvaṟṟiyum,muṉṉum piṉṉum varupavi nāṭi - tattamakku muṉṉum piṉṉum varummoḻikaḷi ārāyntu, otta moḻiyāl puṇarttaṉar uṇarttal -ammoḻikaḷuḷ tamakkup poruntiṉa moḻiyōṭu kūṭip poruḷ uṇarttuka;marapiṉ - aṅṅaṉam uṇarttiya varalāṟṟu muṟimiyāṉē, tattam poruḷmaṉ tōṉṟum - tattamakku uriya poruḷ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum(e-ṟu.)

eṉavē, `uriccol āṇiyāl poruḷ uṇarttātu, varalāṟṟu muṟimiyālporuḷ uṇarttum,' eṉṟār.

(u-m.) `pōkukoṭi maruṅkul' eṉpatil `pōkal' eṉpatu muṉ varumcollāṉ nērmiyi uṇarttiṟṟu.

`tirikāy viṭattaroṭu kāruṭi pōki' eṉpatil `pōkal' eṉpatupiṉvarum collāṉ neṭumiyi uṇarttiṟṟu.

`uṟukāl' eṉpatu muṉvarum `kāl' eṉṉum collāṉ mikutiyiuṇarttiṟṟu.

`aṇaṅkiya cellal' eṉpatu piṉ varum `aṇaṅkiya' eṉṉum collāṉiṉṉāmiyi uṇarttiṟṟu.

iṅṅaṉam kūṟavē,

`muṉṉum piṉṉum moḻiyaṭuttu varutalum'

eṉa iṭiccoṟku ōtiya viti itaṟkum kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu.

TC390n

((uriccol, kūṟappaṭṭa poruḷēyaṉṟip piṟa poruḷum uṇarttal[92))

kūṟiya kiḷavip poruḷ nili alla

vēṟupiṟa tōṉṟiṉum avaṟṟoṭu koḷalē.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.) kūṟiya kiḷavip poruḷ nili alla vēṟu piṟa tōṉṟiṉum -muṉṉum piṉṉum varupavi nāṭiya vaḻi muṟkūṟiya uriccoṟkaḷiṉ poruḷnilimi allāta vēṟupiṟa poruḷ tōṉṟumāyiṉum, avaṟṟoṭu koḷal -akkūṟappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟōṭē avaṟṟiyum uriccollākak koḷka(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `kaṭināṟum pūntuṇar' eṉṟāl, muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiṟku ēlātumaṇapporuṭṭu āyiṟṟu.

`maram puripaṭṭatu,' eṉpatu uyarvuporuḷ kuṟittatu eṉṟukoḷḷaṟka; `pontupaṭṭatu,' eṉṉum poruṭṭu āyiṟṟu.

iviyē aṉṟi, vēṟu piṟa tōṉṟiṉavi avvaccūttiraṅkaḷuḷ kāṭṭippōntām; avaṟṟāṉ uṇarka.

TC391n

((poruḷ uṇarttum muṟimi [93))

poruṭkupporuḷ teriyiṉ atuvaram piṉṟē.

iccūttiram mutal `eḻuttuppirin ticittal' eṉṉumcūttirattaḷavum muṟkūṟiya nālvakic colliyum uṇarumāṟum,uṇarttumāṟum, avaṟṟiṉ taṉmiyum uṇarttukiṉṟār, mēṟkūṟukiṉṟa potuilakkaṇamākiya ecca iyaṟku ēṟpa atikārappaṭṭami karuti.

(i-ḷ.)poruṭkup poruḷ teriyiṉ - oru colli oru collāṉum palacollāṉum uṇarttiyavaḻip poruḷ uṇartta vanta coṟkum poruḷ yātueṉap poruḷ teriyumāyiṉ, atu varampu iṉṟu - aṅṅaṉam teritalvarampiṉṟōṭum; ataṉāl, poruṭkup poruḷ teriyaṟka(e-ṟu.)

oru coṟku oru collāṉum pala collāṉum poruḷ uṇarttiṉālum uṇarumuṇarvu illātāṉi uṇarttumāṟu mēṟkūṟukiṉṟār.

TC392n

((uṇarttumāṟu uṇarttiṉ poruṭkut tiripiṉmi [94))

poruṭkut tiripilli uṇarttavalliṉ.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.)uṇartta valliṉ poruṭkut tiripu illi - māṇākkaṉuṇarumāṟu aṟintu āciriyaṉ uṇarttavallaṉāyiṉ, `iccol ipporuṭṭu,'eṉṟu tāṉ kūṟiyaporuṭkut tiripu iṉṟām(e-ṟu.)

`yām eṉpatu paṭarkki uḷappāṭṭut taṉmip peyar,' eṉṟāluṇarātāṉi, `atu cēymikkaṇ niṉṟāairat taṉṉōṭu kūṭṭip puṟappaṭṭuppuṭipeyaṟci iṉṟi niṉṟatōr poruḷi uṇarttiṟṟuk kāṇ,' eṉat toṭarmoḻi kūṟiyāṉum, cēymikkaṇ niṉṟārit taṉṉōṭu cērttuk kāṭṭiyāṉumporuḷ uṇarttuka.

`uṇṭēm eṉpatu paṉmit taṉmi,' eṉṟāl poruḷ uṇarātāṉi, `ituyāṉum ivaṉum avaṉum uṇṭal toḻilic ceytōm eṉṉum poruḷuṇarttiṟṟukkāṇ,' eṉat toṭarmoḻi kūṟiyāṉum, avarit taṉṉōṭucērttit toḻil nikaḻttik kāṭṭiyāṉum poruḷ uṇarttuka.

``ciṟiyakaṭ peṟiṉē emakkīyu maṉṉē!'

eṉpuḻi maṉṉiccol, `iṉi atu kaḻintatu!' eṉṉum poruḷ kuṟittuniṉṟatu kāṇ,' eṉṟāl poruḷ uṇarātāṉi, `aritākap peṟṟa kaḷḷiekkālamum emakkut tarukiṉṟavaṉ tuṟakkattuc cēṟaliṉ, emakkuk kaḷuṇṭal pōyiṟṟu eṉṟal itaṉ poruḷ,' eṉat toṭarmoḻi kūṟip poruḷuṇarttuka.

``uṟukāl' eṉpuḻi `uṟu' eṉpatu mikuti,' eṉṟāl poruḷ uṇarātāṉi,`kaṭuṅkāliṉatu valikaṇṭāy, īṇṭu `uṟu' eṉpataṟkup poruḷ aḵtu,'eṉṟu toṭar moḻi ##kaṟiyāṉum, kaṭuṅkāl uḷvaḻik kāṭṭiyāṉumuṇarttuka.

iṅṅaṉam nālvakic colliyum māṇākkaṉ uṇarumāṟu aṟintuuṇarttavē poruṭkut tiripu iṉṟām.

iṅṅaṉam uṇarttavum uṇarātāṉi uṇarttaṟpālaṉallaṉ eṉkiṉṟatu mēlvarukiṉṟa cūttiram.

TC393n

((poruḷuṇarcci uṇarvōr āṟṟalip poruttatu [95))

uṇarcci vāyil uṇarvōr valittē.

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.) uṇarcci vāyil - nālvakic colliyum māṇākkaṉ uṇartaṟkukkūṟiya ivvilakkaṇam, uṇarvōr valittu - taṉṉi uṇarvōratu uṇarvittaṉakku vāyilāka uṭittu(e-ṟu.)

eṉavē, taṉṉi uṇarum uṇarvu illātāṟkut tāṉ colvatu payaṉpaṭātuākaliṉ, ataṉi avaṟku uṇarttaṟka eṉpatu poruḷāyiṟṟu.

ittuṇiyum uṇarumāṟum uṇarttumāṟum uṇarttiṉār.

TC394n

((coṟporuḷ uṇarttum kāraṇam āciriyarkkē pulappaṭal [96))

moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā.

itu, coṟporuḷ uṇarttum kāraṇam āciriyarkkē pulappaṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) moḻip poruṭ kāraṇam - nālvakic collukkum poruḷiaṟivittu niṟkiṉṟatōr kāraṇam uṇṭām taṉmi, viḻippat tōṉṟā -nuṇṇuṇarvu illātōrkku `marapu' eṉṟu koḷvatu allatu viḷaṅkattōṉṟā(e-ṟu.)

eṉavē, nuṇṇuṇarvu uṭiyōrkkuk kāraṇam uṇṭām taṉmi viḷaṅkattōṉṟum eṉpatu poruḷāyiṟṟu.

itu marapu eṉṟu kōṭal, `nilantī nīrvaḷi' eṉṉummarapiyaṟcūttirattiṉālum āciriyar uṇarttiṉār.

ulakattup poruḷkaḷiṉ nikaḻccikkuc col kāraṇam ātaliṉ,`moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam' eṉṟār. ikkāraṇam akkāriyaṅkaḷōṭu iyintuniṟkiṉṟa taṉmi āciriyaṟku allatu pulappaṭāmiyiṉ,`viḻippattōṉṟā' eṉṟār.

TC395n

((uriccoṟku eḻuttup pirintu iciyāmi [97))

eḻuttuppirin ticittal ivaṇiyal piṉṟē.

iḵtu, uriccoṟku eḻuttup pirintu icittal iṉṟu eṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)eḻuttup pirintu icittal ivaṇ iyalpu iṉṟu - eḻuttukkaḷmutal niliyum iṟuti niliyumākap pirintu vēṟu vēṟu poruḷuṇarttutal uriccolliṭattu iyalpu uṭittaṉṟu(e-ṟu.)

eṉavē, eḻuttukkaḷ tirintu poruḷ uṇarttutal uriccolliṭattuiyalpu uṭittu eṉṟavāṟām.

eḻuttukkaḷ pirintu icittaṉa viṉiccollum oṭṭuppeyarum.avaṟṟuḷ, viṉi pirintaṉa viṉiiyaluḷ īṟu paṟṟi ōtip pirittukkāṭṭiṉār; peyar pirintaṉa `nammūrntu varūum ikari kāramum'eṉpaṉa mutaliyavaṟṟāl kāṭṭiṉār. `veṟpaṉ, poruppaṉ' eṉpaṉamutalnili pirippap piriyumēṉum, aviyum oṭṭuppeyar eṉṟu uṇarka.

tāmākap poruḷ uṇarttāmiyiṉ, piritalum piriyāmiyum iṭiccoṟkuiṉṟu.

iṉi, uriccol eḻuttut tirintu icittaṉa ellām ivvōttiṉuḷ kāṭṭippōntavaṟṟāṉ uṇarka.

TC396n

((uriccoṟkaḷukkup puṟaṉaṭi [98))

aṉṉa piṟavum kiḷanta alla

paṉmuṟi yāṉum parantaṉa varūum

uriccol ellām poruṭkuṟi kūṭṭa

iyaṉṟa maruṅkiṉ iṉitteṉa aṟiyum

varamputamak kiṉmiyiṉ vaḻinaṉi kaṭippiṭit

tōmpaṭi āṇiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ

pāṅkuṟa uṇartal eṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu, uriccoṟkellām puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) kiḷanta alla aṉṉa piṟavum - collappaṭṭaṉavē aṉṟi avipōlvaṉa piṟavumāy, paṉmuṟiyāṉum parantaṉa varūum uliccol ellām -īṟupaṟṟi uṇarttaṟku aṭaṅkātu palavāṟṟāṉum parantu varum uriccolellām, poruṭkuṟi kūṭṭa - poruḷic col iṉṟiyamiyāk kuṟipāṭutīrap poruḷi ataṉōṭu kūṭṭi uṇartta, iyaṉṟa maruṅkiṉ iṉittu eṉaaṟiyum varampu tamakku iṉmiyiṉ - `ici, kuṟippu, paṇpu' paṟṟittām iyaṉṟa nilattu `ittuṇi' eṉa variyaṟuttu uṇarum ellitamakku iṉmiyāṉ eñcāmal kiḷattal aritu ākaliṉ, vaḻi naṉi kaṭippiṭittu - avaṟṟi aṟivataṟku ōtiya vaḻikaḷic cērāmal mikavumkaṭippiṭittu, ōmpaṭi āṇiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟṟu iyalāṟ pāṅku uṟauṇartal eṉmaṉār pulavar - pātukāvalākiya āṇiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟṟiṉiyalpālē avaṟṟiyum pakutiyuṟa uṇarka eṉṟu colluvarpulavar(e-ṟu.)

`ātalāl yāṉum avvāṟē kūṟiṉēṉ,' eṉṟār eṉak kuṟippeccamākaurikka.

eṉavē,

``peyariṉum viṉiyiṉum meytaṭu māṟi'

eṉavum,

`muṉṉum piṉṉum varupavi nāṭi'

eṉavum yāṉ kūṟiya neṟiyic cērāmal kaṭippiṭittu,

`eccol lāyiṉum poruḷ vēṟu kiḷattal'

eṉavum,

`otta moḻiyāl puṇarttaṉar uṇartal

tattam marapiṉ tōṉṟumaṉ poruḷē'

eṉavum eṉṉāl tarappaṭṭa pātukāvalāṇiyāl kiḷantavaṟṟu iyalālpāṅkuṟa uṇarka,' eṉpatu itaṉ karuttāyiṟṟu.

`uriccol ellām, kūṭṭa varampu tamakku iṉmiyiṉ, kaṭippiṭittu,iyalpākavē avaṟṟi uṇarka,' eṉa viṉi muṭipu ceyka.

`irumi' eṉpatu karumiyum perumiyumākiya kuṟippum, `cēṇ'eṉpatu cēymiyākiya kuṟippum, `toṉmi' eṉpatu paḻamiyākiyakuṟippum uṇarttutalum; `iṟaval' ulōpamum,

`noṟiliyaṟ puravi atiyar kōmāṉ'

eṉa `noṟil' nuṭakkamum,

`noṟiliyaṟ puravik kaḻaṟkāl iḷiyōr'

eṉa `noṟil' virivum, `teviṭṭutal' aṭitalum, `malital'neruṅkutalum, `māli' kuṟṟamum uṇarttutalum; piṟavum `kiḷantaalla' eṉpataṉāl koḷka.

((uri iyal muṟṟum.))

((9. ecca iyal))

TC397n

((nālvakic coṟkaḷ [1))

iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol vaṭacoleṉ
ṟaṉittē ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē.

eṉpatu cūttiram. muṟkūriya eṇvaki ōttiṉuḷḷum uṇarttutaṟkuiṭam iṉṟi eñci niṉṟa col ilakkaṇaṅkaḷit tokuttu uṇarttutaliṉ,iḵtu `ecca iyal' eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. ceyyuṭku uriya collum,avaṟṟu ilakkaṇamum, avaṟṟiṉ vikāramum, acceyyuṭ poruṭkōḷum mutaluṇarttukiṉṟār. iccūttiram, ceyyuḷ viti oḻipu perumpāṉmittuātaliṉ, ceyyuṭ ceytaṟku uriya coṟkaḷ ivi eṉa avaṟṟiṉ variyaṟikūṟukiṉṟatu.

/(i-ḷ.)iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol vaṭacol eṉṟu aṉittē -iyalpākiya collum atutāṉ tirintu varum collum ticikkaṇ vaḻaṅkumcollum āriyac collum eṉṟu kūṟiya attuṇiyē, ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē -oru poruṇmēl nikaḻum ceyyuḷi īṭṭutaṟku uriya collāvaṉa(e-ṟu.)

eṉavē, piṟa pāṭiccoṟkaḷ īṭṭutaṟku uriya aṉṟic ciṟupāṉmivarum āyiṉa. avi, `ceppu, kuḷiru, cikku, antō' eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉapiṟavumām.

iyalpākiya col, muṟkūṟiya nālvakic collēyāy niṉṟu, ceyyuṭkuurittām.

avviyaṟcol, tiricol āṅkāl perumpāṉmi peyarākavum, ciṟupāṉmiviṉittiricollākavum tiritalaṉṟi, iṭiyum uriyumākat tirintuvārā.

ticiccollum vaṭacollum perumpāṉmi peyaṟpeyarāyum ciṟupāṉmitoḻiṟpeyarāyum varutalaṉṟi ēṉiyavāy vārā.

TC398n

((iyaṟcollukku ilakkaṇam [2))

avaṟṟuḷ,

iyaṟcol tāmē

centamiḻ nilattu vaḻakkoṭu civaṇit

tamporuḷ vaḻāmi icikkum collē.

itu, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē iyaṟcoṟku ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)avaṟṟuḷ iyaṟcoltāmē - annāṉkaṉuḷ `iyaṟcol' eṉṟu kūṟiyacoltām, centamiḻ nilattu vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi - centamiḻnilattiṉkaṇṇē vaḻaṅkutalōṭu porunti, tamporuḷ vaḻāmi icikkumcollē - accentamiḻ nilattum koṭuntamiḻ nilattum kēṭṭōrkkut tattamporuḷ vaḻuvāmal olikkum collām (e-ṟu.)

tiripu iṉṟi iyalpākiya collāvaṉa, `nilam, nīr, tī, vaḷi, cōṟu,kūḻ, pāl, makkaḷ, maram, teṅku, kamuku' eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉa.

centamiḻ nilamāvatu, viyi yāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum, maruta yāṟṟiṉteṟkum, karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum, maruvūriṉ mēṟkumām.

TC399n

((tiricollukku ilakkaṇam [3))

oruporuḷ kuṟitta vēṟucol lākiyum

vēṟuporuḷ kuṟitta orucol lākiyum

irupāṟ ṟeṉpa tiricoṟ kiḷavi.

itu, tiricoṟku ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)tiriceṟkiḷavi - avviyaṟcol tirinta tiricollākiya colli,oruporuḷ kuṟitta vēṟucollākiyum vēṟuporuḷ kuṟitta oru collākiyumiru pāṟṟu eṉpa - oru poruḷik kuṟittu varum pala collāyum palaporuḷik kuṟittu varum oru collāyum iru vakippaṭum eṉṟu kūṟuvarāciriyar(e-ṟu.)

avviyaṟcollit tirikkuṅkāl tam eḻuttuc ciṟitu niṟpaṉattirippaṉavum, avviyaṟcoltammiyē piṟacoṟkoṇarntu muḻuvatūumtirippaṉavum eṉa iru vakiyavām.

(u-m.)`kiḷi, mayil' eṉpavaṟṟik `kiḷḷi, maññi' eṉac ciṟituniṟpat tirittum, avaṟṟit `tatti, piṇimukam' eṉap piṟa coṟkoṇarntu muḻuvatūum tirittuṅ kūṟutal.

malikku `veṟpu, vilaṅkal, viṇṭu' eṉpaṉa muḻuvatūum tirittaṉa.ivi oru poruḷ kuṟitta vēṟu col.

`unti' eṉpatu yāḻppattalum, koppūḻum, tērttaṭṭum,kāṉiyāṟṟiṭiyum uṇarttutal muḻuvatūum tirittatu. itu, vēṟu poruḷkuṟitta oru col.

`aḷaku' eṉpatu `kōḻi, kūki, mayil' eṉṉum mūṉṟucātiyiṟpeṇpāṟkum peyarātaliṉ, ituvum vēṟu poruḷ kuṟittatām. ivimuḻuvatūum tirittaṉa.

ivi ellām peyaṟt tiricol.

`kēṭṭīvāyāyiṉ, ceppīmaṉ, īṅkuvantīttāy, pukaḻntikumallarō,eṉmaṉār, eṉṟiciṉōrē, peṟalaruṅkurittu' eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa viṉittiricol. ivi ciṟitu niṟpat tirittaṉa.`piṅkaṇ, pintār,pacciḻi; kārā; cētā' eṉṟāṟpōlum paṇputtokiyākiya coṟkaḷip`pacumi, karumi, cemmi eṉap paṇpuppeyarāka niṟutti,īṟṟuyirmeyyum īṟṟayal uyirum keṭṭu varumoḻikku ēṟpa oṟṟuttirintum tiriyātum āti niṉṟa akaram aikāramāyum tirintaṉa eṉṟum,īṟṟuyir meyyum īṟṟayal uyirum keṭṭu āti nīṇṭatu eṉṟum, īṟṟumeyyumiṭiyil oṟṟum keṭṭu āti nīṇṭatu eṉṟum piṉṉuḷḷōr cantimuṭikkiṉṟatu poruntātu. eṉṉi? `piṅkaṇ' eṉpuḻip `paciyatākiyakaṇ' eṉap paṇpu uṇarttum īṟṟi virittal aṉṟip`pacumikkaṇ' eṉattām tokutta paṇpuppeyari virittal ākāmiyiṉ. ivi ceyyuṭkuppulavar ciṟitu niṟpat tirittukkoṇṭa peyarecca viṉikkuṟippuppeyareṉṟu uṇarka. eḻuttatikārattuc `ceyyuṭku ivvāṟu muṭikka,' eṉaāciriyar muṭipu kūṟiyavaṟṟi ellām `ceyyuḷ muṭipu' eṉṟu kūṟalum,vaḻakkuccol ivvāṟu tirintu vantaṉavaṟṟi `viṉittiricol' eṉṟupeyar kūṟalum āciriyar karuttām.

iṭiyum uriyum tiricollāy varumēṉum uṇarka.

TC400n

((ticiccoṟku ilakkaṇam [4))

centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum

tamkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi.

itu, ticiccoṟku ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) ticiccoṟkiḷavi - ticiccollākiya col, centamiḻ cērntapaṉṉiru nilattum tam kuṟippiṉavē - centamiḻ nāṭṭic cūḻntapaṉṉiraṇṭu nilattum paṉṉiraṇṭiyum puṟam cūḻnta paṉṉiraṇṭunilattum tām tām kuṟitta poruḷiyē viḷakkum taṉmiyiuṭiyaṉa(e-ṟu.)

ummiyi ecca ummiyākkip poruḷ urikka. eṉavē, iyaṟcolpōlaennilattum tam poruḷ viḷakkāvāyiṉa.

paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa: poṅkar nāṭu, oḷi nāṭu, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭu,kuṭṭa nāṭu, kuṭa nāṭu, paṉṟi nāṭu, kaṟkā nāṭu, cīta nāṭu, pūḻināṭu, maliyamāṉāṭu, aruvā nāṭu, aruvāvaṭatali eṉat teṉkīḻpālmutalāka vaṭakīḻpāl īṟāka eṇṇuka.

iṉip paṉṉiraṇṭiyum cūḻnta paṉṉiraṇṭāvaṉa: ciṅkaḷamum,paḻantīvum, kollamum, kūpamum, koṅkaṇamum, tuḷuvum, kuṭakamum,karunaṭamum, kūṭamum, vaṭukum, teluṅkum, kaliṅkamum ām.

teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭār `ā, erumi' eṉpaṉavaṟṟip `peṟṟam' eṉṟum,kuṭṭa nāṭṭār tāyit `taḷḷi' eṉṟum nāyi `ñeḷḷi' eṉṟum,kuṭanāṭṭār tantiyi `accaṉ' eṉṟum, kaṟkā nāṭṭār vañcarik`kiyar' eṉṟum, cīta nāṭṭār `ēṭā' eṉpataṉi `eluvaṉ' eṉṟumtōḻiyi `ikuḷi' eṉṟum `tammāmi' eṉpataṉit `tantuvi' eṉṟum,pūḻi nāṭṭār nāyi `ñamali' eṉṟum ciṟu kuḷattip `pāḻi' eṉṟum,aruvānāṭṭār ceyyic `ceṟu' eṉṟum ciṟukuḷattik `kēṇi' eṉṟum,aruvāvaṭataliyār kuṟuṇiyik `kuṭṭi' eṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa.

iṉic ciṅkaḷam, `antō!' eṉpatu. karunaṭam, `kariya, cikka,kuḷira' eṉpaṉa. vaṭuku, `ceppu' eṉpatu. teluṅku, eruttip`pāṇṭil' eṉpatu. tuḷu, mā marattik `kokku' eṉpatu.

oḻintavaṟṟukkum vantuḻik kāṇka.

TC401n

((vaṭacoṟku ilakkaṇam [5))

vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi

eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē.

itu, vaṭacoṟku ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)vaṭacoṟkiḷavi = vaṭacollākiya col, vaṭaveḻuttu orīi -urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉittum kūṟum vaṭaveḻuttukkaḷiṉ nīṅki,eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collākum - irucār moḻikkum potuvākiya eḻuttāṉiyaṉṟa collām(e-ṟu.)

avi, `vāri, mēru, kuṅkumam, maṇi, māṉam, mīṉam, vīram, vēṇu,kāraṇam, kāriyam, nimittam, kārakam' eṉṟāṟpōlac cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇvaruvaṉa.

iṉi, oḻintōr ceyyuṭkaṇ varuvaṉa, `kamalam, amalam, mūlam,kōpam, ñāṉam, ñēyam, tamālam, tāram, tilakam, nāmam, yāṉam, yōṉi,uṟpalam' ivi pōlvaṉa piṟavumām.

TC402n

((mēlataṟku oru puṟanaṭi [6))

citintaṉa variṉum iyintaṉa variyār

ituvum atu.

(i-ḷ.)citintaṉa variṉum - potu eḻuttāṉ iyaṉṟaṉavē aṉṟivaṭaveḻuttāṉ iyaṉṟa vaṭacol citintu variṉum, iyintaṉa variyār- poruttamuṭiyaṉa ceyyuḷiṭattu nīkkār(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `aramiya viyalakat tiyampum'

`tacanāṉ keytiya paṇimaruḷ nōṉṟāḷ'

`vātiki aṉṉa kavikatir iṟiñci'

`kaṭuntēr irāmaṉ uṭaṉpuṇar cītiyi'

`pēti alli mētiyaṅ kuṟumakaḷ'

`tippami carumattu'

eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ citintu vantaṉa.

iṉi, `tantiram, cūttiram, virutti, aruttāpatti, utti'eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa, nūluḷ citintu vantaṉa. paṅkayam, iṭapam, vicayam,iruṭikaḷ, mēkam, pantam, mayāṉam, viṭam, tuṅkam, palam, pāram,carccari, cāti, cēṉi, citti, cātaṉam, ayaṉ, ari, araṉ, āram,arukam, ciṅkam, mōkkam, kāmam, pakkam, cūlam, catam, pōtakam,pōkam , cuttam, calam, mōkam, yōkam, vantaṉi, vēli, cāli,māli, uvami, vaṉiti, puri, mētiṉi, kumari, nati, araṅkam,ilaṅki, iyakkaṉ, ulōpam, ulōkam, niruttam, kītam, vācciyam,vacciram, aruttam, karumam, karuppam, kāppiyam, paruppatam'eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa piṉṉuḷḷōr ceyyuṭkaṇ tirintu ēṟkum eḻuttukkaḷālmaruvi vantaṉa.

iṉi, `citintaṉa variṉum' eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiya ataṉāl,`āṇi, vaṭṭam, naṭṭam, kaṇṇaṉ' eṉap pātamākac citintaṉavumkoḷka.

TC403n

((ceyyuḷ vikāram [7))

annāṟ collum toṭukkuṅ kāli

valikkumvaḻi valittalum melikkumvaḻi melittalum

virikkumvaḻi virittalum tokukkumvaḻit tokuttalum

nīṭṭumvaḻi nīṭṭalum kuṟukkumvaḻik kuṟukkalum

nāṭṭal valiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.

itu, ceyyuḷ vikāram āmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) annāṟcollum toṭukkuṅkāli - muṟkūṟiya nāṉku colliyumceyyuḷākat toṭukkuṅkāl, valikkumvaḻi valittalum - melloṟṟivalloṟṟākka vēṇṭumvaḻi valleyāṟṟu ākkalum, melikkumvaḻimelittalum - valloṟṟi melloṟṟākka vēṇṭumvaḻi melloṟṟu ākkalum,virikkumvaḻi virittalum - canti niramputaṟku ōr collivirikkavēṇṭumvaḻi virittalum, tokukkumvaḻit tokuttalum - cantamuḷavātaṟku ōr collit tokukkavēṇṭumvaḻit tokuttalum, nīṭṭumvaḻinīṭṭalum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉi nīṭṭavēṇṭumvaḻi nīṭṭalum, kuṟukkumvaḻikkuṟukkalum - neṭṭeḻuttiṉik kuṟukka vēṇṭumvaḻik kuṟukkalumākiyaaṟuvaki vikāramum, nāṭṭal valiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - ceyyuḷ iṉpampeṟac ceyvār nāṭṭutali valiyāka uṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvarpulavar(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`mutti varūum kālam tōṉṟiṉ'

`kuṉṟiya lukarat tiruti ākum.'

`taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ koṭumi'

`iṭiyeṉap paṭupa'

`veḷḷi nalkāḷ vīṭumeṉ uyirē'

`tiruntārnaṉ ṟeṉṟēṉ tiyēṉ.'

eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.

`pācili, aḻuntupaṭu viḻuppuṇ' eṉṟāṟpōla iraṇṭu vikāramvaruvaṉavaṟṟi `nāṭṭal valiya' eṉṟataṉāṟkoḷka.

TC404n

((poruḷkōḷ vaki [8))

niralniṟi cuṇṇam aṭimaṟi moḻimāṟ

ṟavināṉ keṉpa moḻipuṇar iyalpē.

itu, ceyyuḷ ceyvaḻi ataṉ poruṭkiṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)iyalpu puṇarmoḻi - iyaṟkiyākavē tammuḷ kūṭiya poruḷuṇarttum coṟkaḷ aṅṅaṉam iyaṟki yākak kūṭip poruḷ uṇarttātuvikārappaṭṭup poruḷ uṇarttuṅkāl, avi - avvikāra vaki, niralniṟi cuṇṇam aṭimaṟi moḻi māṟṟu nāṉku eṉpa - niral niṟiyumcuṇṇamum aṭi maṟiyum moḻi māṟṟum eṉa nāṉku eṉṟu kūṟuvarāciriyar(e-ṟu.)

niral niṟiyum cuṇṇamum, niralē niṟuttalum tuṇittukkūṭṭutalumākiya vēṟupāṭu uṭimiyiṉ, moḻi māṟṟiṉ vēṟu āyiṉa.

TC405n

((niral niṟip poruḷkōḷ [9))

avaṟṟuḷ,

niralniṟi tāṉē

viṉiyiṉum peyariṉum niṉiyat tōṉṟic

colvēṟu niliip poruḷvēṟu niliyal.

itu, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē `niral niṟi' kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ niral niṟitāṉē - annāṉkaṉuḷ niral niṟiyāvatu,viṉiyiṉum peyariṉum niṉiyat tōṉṟi - viṉiyāṉum peyarāṉumārāyattōṉṟi, col vēṟu niliip poruḷ vēṟu niliyal - muṭikkappaṭumcollum vēṟē nilipeṟṟu muṭikkum collum vēṟē niṟṟalām(e-ṟu.)

toṭarmoḻip poruḷ muṭikkum coṟkaṇṇatu ākalāṉ, muṭikkum collip`poruḷ' eṉṟār.

`viṉiyiṉum peyariṉum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, viṉiccollāṉ varuvatūumpeyarccollāṉ varuvatūum avviru collāṉ varuvatūum eṉa niral niṟimūṉṟām.

(u-m.)`mācu pōkavum kāypaci nīṅkavum

kaṭipuṉal mūḻki aṭicilki toṭṭu'

eṉpuḻi, muṭivaṉavum muṭippaṉavumākiya viṉiccol vēṟu vēṟuniṟṟaliṉ, viṉi niral niṟi.

`koṭikuvaḷi koṭṭi nucuppuṇkaṇ mēṉi'

eṉa muṭivaṉavum muṭippaṉavumākiya peyarccol vēṟu vēṟuniṟṟaliṉ, peyar niral niṟi.`uṭalum uṭintōṭum ūḻmalarum pārkkum

kaṭaliruḷ āmpalpām peṉṟa - keṭalaruñcīrt

tiṅkaḷ tirumukamāc cettu.'

eṉpuḻi, muṭivaṉavākiya peyarum muṭippaṉavākiya viṉiyum vēṟuvēṟu niṟṟaliṉ, potu niral niṟi.

`niṉiya' eṉṟataṉāl, collum poruḷum niralpaṭa nillātu,

`kaḷiṟum kantum pōla naḷikaṭaṟ

kūmpum kalaṉum tōṉṟum'

eṉa mayaṅki varutalum koḷka.

TC406n

((cuṇṇap poruḷkōḷ [10))

cuṇṇan tāṉē

paṭṭāṅ kaminta īraṭi eṇcīr

oṭṭuvaḻi aṟintu tuṇittaṉar iyaṟṟal.

itu, `cuṇṇam' āmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) cuṇṇantāṉē - cuṇṇamāvatu, paṭṭāṅku aminta īraṭi eṇcīr- iyalpāka aminta īraṭikkaṇ uḷavākiya eṇcīri, tuṇittaṉar oṭṭuḻiaṟintu iyaṟṟal - tuṇittu iyiyumvaḻi aṟintu kūṭṭiiyaṟṟappaṭuvatām(e-ṟu.)

īraṭi eṇcīr aḷavaṭi allā vikāra aṭiyāṉum peṟappaṭutaliṉ,avaṟṟi nīkkutaṟku `aḷavaṭiyāṉ aminta' eṉpār, `paṭṭāṅ kamintaīraṭi' eṉṟār.

(u-m.)`curiyāḻa ammi mitappa variyaṉiya

yāṉikku nīttu muyaṟku niliyeṉpa

kāṉaka nāṭaṉ cuṉi.'

`curi mitappa, ammi āḻa, yāṉikku nili, muyaṟku nīttu' eṉattuṇittuk kūṭṭa iyiyum.

cuṇṇampōlac citarntu kiṭattaliṉ, cuṇṇam āyiṟṟu.

itaṉuḷ, `āḻa ammi, nittu muyaṟku' eṉa oṭṭip poruḷ tantuniṉṟaṉavēṉum, curikkum yāṉikkum col muṭipu nōkkuppaṭa niṉṟuporuḷ uṇarttāmiyiṉ, māṟa vēṇṭiṟṟu.

TC407n

((aṭimaṟip poruḷkōḷ [11))

aṭimaṟic ceyti aṭinili tirintu

cīrnili tiriyātu taṭumā ṟummē.

iḵtu, `aṭimaṟi' āmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)aṭi maṟicceyti - `aṭi maṟi' eṉpataṉ ceyti, cīr nilitiriyātu aṭi nili tirintu taṭumāṟum - cīr niṉṟāṅku niṟpa aṭikaḷtattam niliyiṉ tirintu oṉṟaṉ nilikkaḷattu oṉṟu ceṉṟuniṟkum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`māṟāk kātalar malimaṟantaṉarē;

āṟāk kaṭpaṉi varalā ṉāvē;

ēṟā meṉṟōḷ vaḷineki ḻummē;

kūṟāy tōḻi!yāṉ vāḻu māṟē.'

itu cīrum poruḷum tiriyātu, aṭi tirintavāṟu kāṇka. itunāṉkaṭic ceyyuṭkallatu perampāṉmi vārātu.

TC408n

((mēlataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi [12))

poruḷteri maruṅkiṉ

īṟṟaṭi iṟucīr eruttuvayiṉ tiriyum

tōṟṟamum variyār aṭimaṟi yāṉa.

itu, mēlataṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭi.

(i-ḷ.) aṭimaṟiyāṉa - avvaṭimaṟicceyyuḷiṭattu, poruḷ terimaruṅkiṉ - poruḷ muṭivi ārāyumiṭattu, īṟṟu aṭi - apporuḷmuṭiviṉi uṭiya aṭiyil, eruttuvayiṉ iṟucīr - muṭikkiṉṟa cīrkkumutaṟcīriṭattup poruḷē taṉ poruḷāy iṟum cīr, tiriyum tōṟṟamumvariyār - ēṉi aṭikaḷ pōla nērāy muṭiyātu mīṇṭu ceṉṟu muṉniṉṟatōr collōṭē poruḷ muṭiyum tōṟṟaravum nīkkār āciriyar(e-ṟu.)

`kūṟāy tōḻi!yāṉ vāḻu māṟē.'

eṉpatu, yāṇṭu niṟpiṉum pāṭṭil poruḷ muṭikkiṉṟa aṭi. atil`vāḻum' eṉpatu, eruttuc cīr. `māṟu' eṉṉum iṭiccollākiyaiṟuticcīr tāṉ pirintu niṉṟu uṇarttutaṟkut taṉakku ōr poruḷ iṉṟi`vāḻumpaṭiyi' eṉa eruttuccīriṉ poruḷiyē uṇartti, urupi ēṟṟuk`kūṟāy' eṉa muṉ niṉṟa collōṭu muṭintavāṟu kāṇka.

ummiyāṉ, iṅṅaṉam muṭital ciṟupāṉmi āyiṟṟu.

TC409n

((moḻi māṟṟup poruḷkōḷ [13))

moḻimāṟ ṟiyaṟki

colnili māṟṟip poruḷetir iyiya

muṉṉum piṉṉum koḷvaḻik koḷāal.

itu, `moḻi māṟṟu' āmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)moḻi māṟṟu iyaṟki - moḻi māṟṟiṉatu iyalpāvatu, colnilip poruḷ etir iyiya muṉṉum piṉṉum māṟṟi - ceyyuṭkaṇ `pā'eṉṉum uṟuppiṟku ēṟpac col niṉṟa nili poruḷ tārāta vaḻic colniṉṟa nilikkup poruḷ etirē vantu poruntumpaṭi accolliṉ muṉ niṉṟacollip piṉṉēyum piṉ niṉṟa colli muṉṉēyum māṟṟi, koḷvaḻik koḷāal- poruḷ koḷḷumiṭattē koḷuttutalām(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.) `kuraṅkukuḷip polinta keycuvaṟ puravi

narampārp paṉṉa vāṅkuvaḷ pariya'

`puraviṉatu vāṅkuvaḷ narampu ārppu aṉṉa pariya' eṉa muṉ niṉṟacollip piṉṉē māṟṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. iccūttiramum atu.

/`iṭimulik kiṭantu'

eṉpatu, `muliyiṭi' eṉap piṉ niṉṟa moḻiyi muṉṉē māṟṟiyavāṟukāṇka.

`kaṭaṟpaṭi kuḷippa maṇṭi'

eṉpatum atu.

`irumpu tirittaṉṉa māyiru maruppiṟ

paralaval aṭiya irali terippa'

eṉpatum atu.

`poṉṟōṭip pukaraṇinutal

tuṉṉaruntiṟal kamaḻkaṭāat

teyiṟupaṭi yāka veyiṟkata viṭāak

kayiṟupiṇik koṇṭa kaviḻmaṇi maruṅkiṟ

peruṅki yāṉiyirum piṭarttali iruntu

maruntil kūṟṟat taruntoḻil cāvā'

eṉṟārpōla varuvaṉa ellām `aṇuki vanta māṭṭu' eṉṉum uṟuppu.

`āriya maṉṉar paṟiyiṉ eḻuntiyampum'

eṉpatum atu.

`akaṉṟu vanta māṭṭu' murukāṟṟuppaṭi mutaliyavaṟṟuṭkāṇka.

moḻimāṟṟāvatu, kēṭṭōr kūṭṭi uṇarumāṟṟāṉ īraṭikkaṇṇē varuvatueṉṟum; `māṭṭu' eṉṉum uṟuppāvatu, iraṇṭu iṟanta pala aṭikkaṇṇumpala ceyyuḷ toṭariṉkaṇṇum akaṉṟum aṇukiyum varum eṉṟum uṇarka.aḵtu,

`akaṉṟuporuḷ kiṭappiṉum aṇukiya niliyiṉum

iyaṉṟuporuḷ muṭiyat tantaṉar uṇarttal

māṭṭeṉa moḻipa pāṭṭiyal vaḻakkiṉ.'

eṉṉum ceyyuḷiyaṟcūttirattāṉ uṇarka. utāraṇaṅkaḷ āṇṭukkāṭṭutum.

pūṭṭuviṟporuḷkōḷum, aḷimaṟi pāppupporuḷkōḷum, tāppicipporuḷkōḷum, koṇṭukūṭṭup poruḷkēḷum `māṭṭu' eṉṉum uṟuppiṉkaṇaṭaṅkumāṟum; yāṟṟoḻukkup poruḷkōḷ `yāppu' eṉṉum uṟuppiṉkaṇaṭaṅkumāṟum uṇarka.

TC410n

((kiḷippeyarkaḷ pirikkappaṭā [14))

ta na nu eeṉum avimuta lākiya

kiḷi nutaṟ peyarum pirippap piriyā.

itu, mēl tokiccoṟkūṟuvāṉ, tokipōla iru poruḷpaṭuvaṉa ivieṉkiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) ta na nu e eṉum avi mutalākiya kiḷi nutaṟ peyarum -`ta, na, nu, e' eṉkiṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷi mutalāka uṭiya oruvarōṭuoruvarkku uḷḷa toṭarccikkiḻamiyik karutiṉa oṭṭuppeyarkaḷum,pirippap piriyā - tokiccoṟpōla iru poruḷpaṭṭu niṟpiṉum avipōlap piṉ varum col tam poruḷ uṇarttāmiyiṉ pirikkappaṭā,orucollēyāy niṟkum(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)tamaṉ, tamaḷ, tamar.

namaṉ, namaḷ, namar.

numaṉ, numaḷ, numar.

emaṉ, emaḷ, emar.

eṉa varum. viḷi marapil mutalum īṟum paṟṟi ōti iṭiyiṉvikaṟpam kūṟāmiyiṉ, `tammāṉ, nammāṉ, nummāṉ, emmāṉ' eṉṟāṟpōlumvāyppāṭṭu vikaṟpamum koḷka.

cēṉāvariyar, `taṅkiḷi, naṅkiḷi, nuṅkiḷi, eṅkiḷi'eṉpaṉavaṟṟi orumikkum paṉimakkum potuvāy niṟkum eṉṟārāl eṉiṉ,āciriyar,

`tāmeṉ kiḷavi paṉimak kurittē.'

`yām nāmeṉa varūum peyarum'

eṉa ivvatikārattu eṭuttu ōtiya paṉmic coṟkaḷieḻuttatikārattuḷ avi eḻuvāyum payaniliyumāy niṟkumvaḻittiriyātu puṇarum eṉṟu kūṟi,

`tāmnām eṉṉum makara iṟutiyum'

eṉṉum cūttirattāṉ urupu ēṟkumvaḻit tirintu puṇarum eṉṟu kūṟi,`num' eṉpataṉi,

`nummeṉ orupeyar'

eṉṉum cūttirattāṉ urupu ēṟkumāṟu kūṟi,

`allataṉ maruṅkiṉ'

eṉṉum cūttirattāṉ aḵtu eḻuvāyum payaniliyumāy niṟkumvaḻi`nīyir' eṉat tirintu paṉmiyāyē niṟkum eṉavum kūṟippōntār ātaliṉ,īṇṭum ataṟku ēṟpat `tammuṭiya kiḷi, emmuṭiya kiḷi' eṉappaṉmi uṇarttutal aṉṟi orumi uṇarttāmi uṇarka. ēṉiyavum aṉṉa.

`tamporuḷ eṉpatam makkaḷ'

eṉavum,

`namma ṉōrkkē'

eṉavum,

`numma ṉōrumaṟ ṟiṉiya rāyiṉ

emma ṉōrivaṇ piṟavalar mātō'

eṉavum innāṉku collum ammuṭipu peṟṟu, mutal nili tirintuniṉṟu, paṉmipporuḷ uṇarttiyavāṟu kāṇka.

īṇṭu ivi `veṟpaṉ, poruppaṉ' pōlat `tam, nam, num, em' eṉpaṉapirintu niṉṟu poruḷ uṇarttip piṉ varukiṉṟa `aṉ, aḷ, ar' eṉpaṉaporuḷ uṇarttātu iṭiccollāyē niṟṟaliṉ, `pirippap piriyā,' eṉṟār.

iṉi, ummiyi eccappaṭuttip `piṟa oṭṭup peyarkaḷum pirippappiriyā,' eṉap poruḷ kūṟi, `impar, umpar; immi, ummi, emmi'eṉṟārpōlvaṉa piṟavum `ivviṭam, uvviṭam; ippiṟappu, uppiṟappu,eppiṟappu' eṉṉum poruḷ uṇartti niṟkum eṉṟu koḷka.

TC411n

((oru col aṭukku [15))

iciniṟi acinili poruḷoṭu puṇartaleṉ

ṟavimūṉ ṟeṉpa orucol aṭukkē.

ituvum tokiccoṟpōla aṭukki varuvaṉa kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.)oru col aṭukkē - oru colli aṭukkum aṭukku, iciniṟiacinili poruḷoṭu puṇartal eṉṟu avi mūṉṟu eṉpa - iciniṟiyumaaicaniliyum poruḷoṭu puṇartalum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avi mūṉṟumeṉṟu colluvar āciriyar(e-ṟu.)

(u-m.)`ēē ēē ampal moḻintaṉaḷ.' _ iḵtu ici niṟi.

`maṟṟō maṟṟō' _ iḵtu acinili.

`vantatu vantatu kūṟṟu.' avaṉ avaṉ, vitēṉ vitēṉ, pōm pōmeṉpaṉa virivum, tuṇivum, uṭampāṭum, orutoḻil palakālnikaḻtalumākiya poruḷ vēṟupāṭu uṇarttaliṉ, poruḷoṭu puṇartal.

((toki moḻiyiṉ vaki [16))

vēṟṟumit tokiyē uvamat tokiyē

viṉiyiṉ tokiyē paṇpiṉ tokiyē

ummit tokiyē aṉmoḻit tokiyeṉ

ṟavvā ṟeṉpa tokimoḻi niliyē.

itu, tokiccoṟkup peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum kūṟukiṉṟatu.

(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumit tokiyē - vēṟṟumi urupukaḷ tokka toki,uvamattokiyē - uvama urupukaḷ tokka toki, viṉiyiṉ tokiyē -viṉiccol īṟukaḷ tokka toki, paṇpiṉ tokiyē - paṇpu uṇarttumīṟukaḷ tokka toki, ummit tokiyē - `um' eṉṉum iṭiccol tokkatoki, aṉmoḻittoki - ittokic coṟkaḷiṉ iṟutikkaṇ vantu poruḷtarum vēṟōr col tokka toki, eṉṟu avvāṟu eṉpa - eṉṟu collappaṭṭaavvāṟum eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar, toki moḻi niliyē - tokiccolliṉatu nilimiyi(e-ṟu.)

`aimpāl aṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum'

eṉavum,

`ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum kiḷaviyiṉ

meyyoruṅ kiyalum toḻiltoku moḻiyum'

eṉavum,

`uruputeka varutalum'

eṉavum,

`meyyurupu tokāa iṟuti yāṉa

eṉavum,

`paṇputeka varūum kiḷavi yāṉum'

eṉavum,

`vēṟṟumi tokka peyarvayi ṉāṉum'

`ummi eñciya irupeyart tokimoḻi'

eṉavum āciriyar tokkē niṟkum eṉac cūttiram ceytaliṉ, vēṟṟumiuṟupum uvama urupum ummum viṉāccol īṟum paṇpu uṇarttum īṟumittokiccoṟkaḷ allātatōr collum tekku niṟṟaliṉ, tokiccol eṉpatēavar karuttāyiṟṟu. `āyiṉ, `kēḻaṟpaṉṟi, vēḻakkarumpu'eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟkut tokkaṉa iṉmiyiṉ, tokiccol āmāṟu eṉṉi?' eṉiṉ,avaṟṟiṟkum oṉṟi oṉṟu vicēṭittu niṟkiṉṟa taṉmiyi uṇarttutaṟkuvarum `ākiya' eṉṉum vācakam tokku niṉṟatu eṉṟē kōṭum. aṉṟit`taṉkaṇ tokkaṉa iṉṟi aṅṅaṉam vācakam tokku niṉṟatu `toki'eṉṟaṟkuc cālāmiyiṉ, ataṉi oṭṭi oru collāy niṉṟu tam poruḷuṇarttum eṉṟu koṇṭu ēṉiyavaṟṟiyum avvāṟē kōṭum,' eṉiṉ,eṭuttōttiṉum ilēcu ciṟattal yāṇṭum iṉmiyiṉ, atu poruntātu.eṉṉi? avvirupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtoki aṟuvakit tokiccūttiraṅkaḷuḷ eṭuttōttu iṉṟi `eṉṉa kiḷaviyum' eṉṟa ilēciṉālkoṇṭatākaliṉ. āyiṉ, `vaṇṇattiṉ vaṭiviṉ' eṉpataṉkaṇ irupeyaroṭṭāyaimpāl aṟiyāta vaṭiviṉiyum eṭuttōtiṉārāl eṉiṉ, vaṭivumutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum aṟuvakit tiripiṉuḷ ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu muṭittaliṉ,kuṉṟalum tokiyām eṉṟu uṇarka. `eṉṉa kiḷaviyum' eṉṟataṉāl koḷvaṉavarumoḻi iṉṟi nilimoḻiyum poruḷ uṇartti niṟkum eṉpatu āṇṭukkūṟutum.

aṉṟiyum, `ceytāṉ poruḷ, iruntāṉ kuṉṟattu' eṉa vaḻaṅkuvaṉa oṭṭioru col ākāmiyum; ceyyuṭkaṇ

`kaḻūuviḷaṅ kāram kaviiya mārpē'

eṉavum,

`māṟṟumik koṇṭa vaḻi'

eṉavum iṟutikkaṇ iraṇṭāvatu tokkaṉa,

`muyaṅkiṉe ṉallaṉō'

eṉavum, `pōṟṟāy' eṉavum varum muṭikkum coṟkaḷōṭu oṭṭi orucolākāmiyum;

`tōṉṟal maṟantōr tuṟikeḻu nāṭṭē'

eṉavum,

`ciṟukural neytalem peruṅkaḻi nāṭṭē'

eṉavum iṟutikkaṇ ēḻāvatu tokkaṉa, `tōṉṟum' eṉavum `cērntaṉiceliṉē' eṉavum varum muṭikkum coṟkaḷōṭu oṭṭi oru col ākāmiyumuṇarka.

iṉṉum,

`vallē maṟaval ōmpumati emmē.'

eṉa iṭikkaṇ tokkaṉavum

`poṉṉōṭip pukaraṇinutal

tuṉṉaruntiṟal kamaḻkaṭāat

teyiṟupaṭi yāka eyiṟkata viṭāak

kayiṟupiṇik koṇṭa kaviḻmaṇi maruṅkiṟ

peruṅki yāṉi'

eṉa aṭukki varuvaṉavum oṭṭi oru col ākāmiyum uṇarka.

`atuveṉ vēṟṟumi' eṉṉum cūttirattu `atuveṉ urupukeṭa'eṉṟataṉāl, `atu' eṉ uṟupu niṉṟāl allatu keṭutal iṉmiyiṉ, atuniṉṟu keṭṭatu eṉṉum poruḷ taral āṇṭuk kūṟip pōntām. ataṉāl, īṇṭuccēṉāvariyar kūṟiya poruṭku viṭi iṉmi uṇarka.

ivaṟṟuḷ viṉittokiyum paṇpiṉ tokiyum ciṟitu tokkuttokiyātalum, ēṉiya muḻuvatūum tokkut tokiyātalum uṇarka.
